# Tests, explained

Every test in the workspace, grouped by file. Run all with `cargo test --workspace` (must pass before any task is done — rule R7.1).

Fixtures shared by the server and CLI tests live in `crates/kaanal-server/tests/fixtures/`: `kaanal.yaml` (gateway `nginx` → service `app` → database `db`, all 1 ms costs, seed 42, two `endpoints:` overrides), `openapi.yaml` (users/orders spec), `openapi-warn.yaml` (spec containing an ignored `not` keyword). The shipped examples `examples/nginx-server` and `examples/gatewayless` (no gateway) are validated separately.

---

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/config/` — YAML parsing

One test target (`cargo test -p kaanal-core --test config`), one module per topic in `tests/config/` (R3.8):

| Module         | What it covers                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `common.rs`    | The sample config.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `draft.rs`     | `kaanal draft`: `draft_show_prints_the_config_text_the_instance_started_from` (text and `--json`), `draft_validate_prints_positions_and_exits_1` (`--file` is checked without storing, the stored draft prints as `draft:LINE:`), `draft_apply_prints_kept_dropped_and_flagged` (kept chaos, a dropped active scenario, a flagged scenario file, the new system serving, the draft clean at generation 2), `draft_apply_with_a_bad_draft_exits_1_with_the_position_and_changes_nothing`, `draft_commands_honour_json_and_the_timeout_flag` (a bad or over-60s `--timeout` is a usage error), `draft_save_prints_the_files_written` (nothing on disk until save, `saved: kaanal.yaml`, `--json`), `draft_save_refuses_when_the_file_changed_and_removes_stale_scenarios_on_request` (a file edited behind the instance is exit 1 and kept; `--fix-scenarios remove` deletes the flagged file and prints it; an unknown value is a usage error). `show`, `validate` and `apply` are also in `every_client_command_honours_json`. |
| `basics.rs`    | Defaults, positioned errors, durations.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `nodes.rs`     | Node kinds, p50 and p99, edges, the schema.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `endpoints.rs` | The `endpoints:` map and its positioned errors.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `auth.rs`      | Claims, scopes, users, passwords, token ttl.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `limits.rs`    | Concurrency, rate limits, capacity checks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `locale.rs`    | Locale tags.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| Test                                                         | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `parses_sample`                                              | A full sample file lands in the right typed fields: seed, port, node kinds, `spec`, edges, route override default status 200, and `proc: 5ms` parses as `Fixed(5ms)`.                                                      |
| `defaults_apply`                                             | Minimal config (`version: 1, nodes: {}`) gets seed 0, port 8080, empty edges.                                                                                                                                              |
| `bad_value_reports_line`                                     | A type error deep in the file reports the right **line number** and renders as `3: …` (rule R6.1).                                                                                                                         |
| `unknown_field_is_rejected`                                  | `deny_unknown_fields`: a `bogus:` key fails and names itself.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `wrong_version_is_rejected`                                  | `version: 2` fails with a version message.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `bom_and_crlf_parse_like_lf`                                 | UTF-8 BOM + Windows CRLF line endings parse identically (rule R6.4).                                                                                                                                                       |
| `bad_duration_explains_what_is_expected`                     | Bad `proc` values (in all three duration shapes) fail with the friendly "expected a duration like …" message and the right line — not serde's untagged-enum gibberish.                                                     |
| `huge_durations_are_rejected_with_a_position`                | A duration or p99 over 1 h is rejected with its line (summed hops would overflow); exactly 1 h is fine. `p50` above `p99` is rejected (it would become an uncapped fixed value); `p50 == p99` is fine.                     |
| `exponential_duration_parses_and_is_strict`                  | `{ mean: 5ms }` parses; `{ mean: 2h }` is rejected with its line; `{ mean, p99 }` is rejected with the "expected a duration like" message.                                                                                 |
| `concurrency_parses_on_every_node_type_and_must_be_positive` | `concurrency` as a number parses on gateway/service/database and is carried into `Node.admission` (unbounded queue); `0`/`-1`/`1.5` are rejected with line 3; absent = unlimited.                                          |
| `concurrency_takes_a_number_or_a_map`                        | `concurrency` is a number or `{ limit, queue, overflow_status }`; the map defaults to queue 0 and status 503 like `pool`; `slots()` reads either form.                                                                     |
| `bad_concurrency_values_are_placed_and_named`                | Eight bad `concurrency` values (0, negative, fraction, text, `limit: 0`, no `limit`, status 418, unknown key) each fail on line 3 with a message that names the problem.                                                   |
| `rate_limit_parses_on_every_node_kind`                       | `rate_limit: { rps, burst }` parses on all seven kinds.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `capacity_limits_are_checked_at_load`                        | `pool` plus `concurrency` on one node fails at the node; `rps` over 1e9 and a `burst / rps` window over a year fail at the limit; the allowed extremes still load.                                                         |
| `all_seven_kinds_parse_with_every_field`                     | One node of each kind with every field parses into its own struct (pool defaults, rate limit, users, ttl, proxy), and the accessors agree.                                                                                 |
| `unknown_type_lists_the_kinds`                               | `type: router` fails at line 3 and lists all seven kinds.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `fields_belong_to_their_kind`                                | A field on the wrong kind (`ops` on a service, `spec` on a gateway, `proc` on a database, `pool` on a cache, `flavor` on auth) is an `unknown field` error.                                                                |
| `p50_above_p99_is_rejected_with_a_position`                  | `{ p50, p99 }` with p50 above p99 fails with "above p99" at line 3, including the `100h` case that used to slip past the 1 h cap; equal percentiles are fine.                                                              |
| `inert_fields_are_validated`                                 | Capacity fields and the still-inert fields are validated: pool size and status range, rate-limit rps and burst, error rate, proxy URL, hit ratio, token TTL cap, duplicate usernames, missing password.                    |
| `edge_calls_default_to_one_and_reject_zero`                  | `calls` defaults to 1, takes 3, and `calls: 0` fails at the edge's line.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `node_kind_names_match_yaml`                                 | `NodeKind::as_str()` and `Display` give the YAML spelling (`external-api`, `monolith`).                                                                                                                                    |
| `keyword_named_nodes_load`                                   | Nodes named `true`, `null`, `yes`, `on`, `1` load under their literal name.                                                                                                                                                |
| `schema_has_one_branch_per_kind`                             | The JSON Schema has one `$defs.NodeConfig.oneOf` branch per kind, in order, each with `additionalProperties: false` (diagnostics rely on this layout).                                                                     |
| `routes_key_is_replaced_by_endpoints`                        | A file with `routes:` fails at the key with a pointer to `endpoints:`.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `serde_errors_do_not_repeat_their_position_or_offer_routes`  | A serde error message has no trailing "at line L column C" (the position is in the error), and an unknown top-level key does not suggest the removed `routes`; same for endpoints-file shape errors.                       |
| `endpoints_parse_inline_with_every_step_shape`               | An inline `endpoints:` entry reads `op`, `override`, `error_map` (status, class and `{ status, body }`) and every step shape (cache, call with chain/with/via, bare names, parallel branches); node `chains` parse.        |
| `step_shape_errors_are_placed_at_the_step`                   | Malformed steps (extra fields on cache/parallel, no kind, unknown or duplicate field, negative repeat, a list as a step) fail at serde's own mark.                                                                         |
| `endpoint_entries_are_strict`                                | Unknown entry fields, duplicate endpoint keys, a non-path non-map `endpoints:`, a bad `error_map` value and a bad `determinism` all fail with a position.                                                                  |
| `chains_belong_to_services_and_monoliths`                    | `chains:` loads on a monolith and is an unknown field on a database.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `endpoints_file_parses_and_places_shape_errors`              | `parse_endpoints` reads a map, treats empty/comment-only/BOM-only files as no entries, places step errors in the file and gives the same position for CRLF plus BOM.                                                       |
| `auth_claims_and_scopes_parse`                               | An auth node's `claims`, `token_ttl` and users' `roles`, `scopes` and `claims` land in their typed fields; omitted ones are empty.                                                                                         |
| `reserved_claim_on_a_later_user_points_at_that_claim`        | A reserved claim (`iss`) on user #57 is reported at that claim's key (63:49), not at `users:`.                                                                                                                             |
| `auth_rules_are_checked_after_parsing_and_placed`            | Reserved/typed/oversized claims, too many claims/roles/users, empty username, long password, bad or empty scope: each fails at its own key or value with a named path; no message echoes a value; 1000 users load.         |
| `a_non_text_password_is_rejected_at_its_value_without_echo`  | A password written as a number, `true`, a float or `null` is rejected with `must be text, not …` at the value (6:34 flow, 7:19 block) and the message never contains the value; `"4711"`, `0123` and `007` load unchanged. |
| `token_ttl_must_be_whole_seconds_of_at_least_one`            | `1500ms`, `0s`, `500ms` fail at the value (5:16); `1s`, `90s`, `24h` load.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `user_debug_hides_the_password`                              | `Debug` of a config shows the username and `<redacted>`, never the password.                                                                                                                                               |
| `node_locale_and_project_locale_parse_and_default`           | Top-level `locale:` and `data:` and a per-node `locale:` parse (every node kind); an absent one is `None` (the server applies `en-IN`); the topology node carries it.                                                      |
| `locale_tags_must_match_the_pattern_before_any_path_use`     | `valid_locale_tag` accepts `en`, `en-IN`, `zh-Hant-TW`, `gsw-CH` and rejects `../../x`, `a/b`, `en_IN`, the empty tag and a trailing or leading hyphen (D1.4).                                                             |
| `a_bad_locale_tag_is_an_error_at_its_value`                  | A bad project or node `locale:` fails at the value's line and column, naming the node when it is one.                                                                                                                      |
| `prob_parses_on_a_step_and_an_edge`                          | (`prob.rs`) `prob: 0.5` on an edge and `{ call: db, prob: 0.2 }` on a step parse into `EdgeConfig::prob` and `CallStep::prob`.                                                                                             |
| `an_edge_without_prob_has_none`                              | An edge with no `prob` reads as `None` (1.0 once built).                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `prob_must_be_in_0_exclusive_to_1`                           | `0`, `-0.5`, `1.5` and `.nan` fail at the value's line; `1` loads.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `prob_on_an_edge_to_a_cache_is_rejected`                     | The message says a cache keeps its own hit ratio.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `prob_on_a_gateway_edge_is_rejected`                         | The message says the gateway routes by endpoint.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `a_cache_step_rejects_prob`                                  | `{ cache: c, prob: 0.5 }` fails: a cache step takes no other fields.                                                                                                                                                       |

| `replicas_range_is_1_to_64` | (`replicas.rs`) 0, 65 and 1000 fail at the value's line with the range message; 1 and 64 load. |
| `balance_parses_its_three_forms` | `round-robin`, `random` and `{ weighted: [3, 1, 1] }` parse; an absent `balance` reads as `None`. |
| `a_bad_balance_says_what_is_expected` | `sticky`, a bare `weighted`, a list and a map with an extra key are errors that name the valid forms. |
| `weighted_length_must_equal_replicas` | Two weights for three replicas fails at the `balance` value. |
| `weighted_needs_one_positive_weight` | `[0, 0]` fails; `[0, 5]` loads. |
| `balance_without_replicas_is_accepted_and_inert` | `balance: random` with no `replicas` loads; the topology has 1 replica and keeps the policy. |
| `an_edge_balance_overrides_the_node_and_is_checked_against_the_callee` | An edge `balance` reaches `Edge::balance`; a weighted list of the wrong length for the callee fails at the edge. |
| `replicas_parse_on_every_node_kind` | `replicas` and `balance` parse on all seven kinds. |
| `bandwidth_must_be_positive` | (`payload.rs`) `bandwidth: 0` fails at its value's line with the minimum; `bandwidth: 1000000` loads. |
| `units.rs` (module) | F7: `suffixes_load_to_the_same_values_as_integer_bytes` (node, edge and journey, compared field by field and as a topology), `every_spelling_is_accepted_in_yaml`, `a_bound_still_fails_after_conversion` (`0KB`, `0 MB/s`, `req_bytes: 2GB`; `1GB` loads), `refusals_are_positioned_and_name_the_field`, `a_scenario_cap_takes_a_unit_like_the_yaml`, `the_schema_takes_a_number_or_a_unit_text`. The parser itself has table tests in `src/units.rs` (`accepted_forms`, `refusals_name_the_problem_and_the_units`). |
| `per_kb_parses_as_a_duration_like_proc_on_every_kind_but_a_cache` | `per_kb: 50us` parses on all seven kinds (and builds into a fixed `Dist` on the four that can be an entry); `{ mean: 2ms }` builds an exponential. |
| `per_kb_on_a_cache_is_rejected` | A cache with `per_kb` fails at its value: it has no per-body processing, use `bandwidth` on its edge. |
| `an_edge_target_parses_as_from_to_fields` | (`link_chaos.rs`) `edge: { from, to }` with latency, jitter and a cap resolves to an `Edge` layer and compiles to `link(0)`. || `a_status_kind_on_a_link_is_rejected` | `error_kind` status or malformed and `error_status` on an edge fail; reset and hang pass, and the default is reset. || `bandwidth_cap_without_edge_bandwidth_is_rejected` | A cap on an edge with no bandwidth, or above it, fails; at it passes. || `a_scenario_naming_a_missing_edge_is_a_positioned_load_error` | `no edge 'gw' -> 'db'` at `chaos.0.edge`. || `link_fields_and_node_targets_do_not_mix` | `jitter_ms` or `bandwidth_cap` on a node entry, and an edge with a node, are rejected. || `edge_layers_merge_like_node_layers` | Latency adds, the largest jitter and the smallest cap win, and an override drops the lower layer. |
| `payload_costs_is_false_by_default_and_true_with_either_field` | A topology with neither field has `payload_costs() == false`, zero `per_kb` and no `bandwidth`; either field turns it true. |
| `edge_id_is_the_config_index` | `Topology::edge_id` returns the config index of an edge, `None` for a pair with no edge. |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/diagnostics.rs` — error positions

| Test                                                        | What it proves                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `unknown_field_in_the_second_node_is_reported_at_its_key`   | An unknown field in the second node is placed at its key, listing the valid fields.                                                                                 |
| `spike_cases_point_at_the_problem`                          | Missing nested field, bad value, bad duration, nested unknown field, unknown type and missing type each get the right line:col and node name.                       |
| `comments_crlf_and_bom_do_not_move_positions`               | Comments shift lines as expected; CRLF + BOM give the same error as LF.                                                                                             |
| `errors_outside_nodes_keep_serdes_position`                 | An error before `nodes:` keeps serde's line even if a node below is bad.                                                                                            |
| `anchors_and_merge_keys_fall_back_cleanly`                  | `<<` merge keys are reported as unknown fields; an undefined alias is an error, not a panic.                                                                        |
| `nodes_named_like_yaml_keywords_are_found`                  | Nodes named `true`, `null`, `yes`, `on`, `1` are located.                                                                                                           |
| `graph_errors_point_at_the_edge_or_node`                    | Forbidden edge, cycle, unknown node name, second gateway and empty topology are placed at the edge, name, node key or `nodes`.                                      |
| `located_without_a_place_has_no_position`                   | An empty or unmatched path gives no position; a version error is placed at `version`.                                                                               |
| `explain_never_panics_or_flips_the_verdict`                 | Mutation sweep (line deletions, truncations, duplicates, indents, `;`) plus odd shapes: no panic, serde-accepted input stays accepted, reported lines are in range. |
| `huge_source_falls_back_to_serdes_position`                 | A source over 1 MiB (or with a line over 8 KiB) still returns serde's positioned error.                                                                             |
| `deep_block_nesting_does_not_overflow_the_stack`            | 500k nested block sequences on one line fall back to serde's error instead of building a huge tree.                                                                 |
| `scalars_serde_reads_as_strings_do_not_hide_the_real_error` | `password: 12345` is valid to serde; the real error in a later node is still the one reported.                                                                      |
| `located_points_at_values_and_keeps_known_positions`        | `Located::at_value` points at a value, a known `pos` is kept as is, `in_file` and `parsed` set the file.                                                            |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/schema.rs` — the published schema

| Test                                          | What it proves                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `committed_schema_matches_the_types`          | `kaanal.schema.json` equals `config_schema()` as JSON values; `KAANAL_BLESS=1` rewrites it.                                             |
| `shipped_configs_validate_against_the_schema` | Every `examples/*/kaanal.yaml` and the server fixture validate against the schema.                                                      |
| `every_schema_field_has_a_description`        | Every property in the schema has a non-empty description (a doc comment).                                                               |
| `inert_fields_say_they_have_no_effect_yet`    | The accepted-but-unused fields (`error_rate`, `proxy`, `ttl`) say "no effect yet"; the auth fields (`users`, `token_ttl`) no longer do. |
| `concurrency_schema_is_a_number_or_a_map`     | `concurrency` is `anyOf` an integer of at least 1 or the `BoundedConcurrency` map, and every kind has `rate_limit`.                     |
| `schema_describes_endpoints_and_steps`        | `routes` is gone; `endpoints`, `determinism`, `Step` and service `chains` exist; sample endpoints (inline and file path) validate.      |
| `schema_describes_auth_claims_and_scopes`     | Auth `claims` is an object of string/number/boolean values; `UserConfig` has `scopes` and `claims`; a sample with them validates.       |
| `schema_rejects_rates_the_loader_rejects`     | The published schema accepts `rate_limit.rps` of 1000000000 and rejects 2000000000 and 0, matching the loader.                          |
| `schema_bounds_prob_like_the_loader`          | The schema accepts `prob` of 0.5 and 1 and rejects 0 and above 1, on an edge and on a `call:` step, matching the loader.                |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/presets.rs` — flavor presets

| Test                                               | What it proves                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `flavor_fills_missing_ops_and_user_values_win`     | A flavor fills the ops the user left out and the user's op wins; through `Topology::build`, a database with only a flavor gets the whole preset.      |
| `without_flavor_all_three_ops_are_needed`          | With no flavor, partial or absent `ops` resolve to nothing (topology reports `MissingOps`); all three given are used as is.                           |
| `unknown_or_wrong_family_flavor_lists_valid_names` | `redis` on a database, `oracle`, `memcached` on a monolith and `postgres` on a cache each fail at line 3 listing the valid names of the right family. |
| `cache_flavor_fills_the_gaps`                      | A cache flavor fills `hit`/`miss` while the user's `hit_ratio` wins; without a flavor a missing `hit_ratio` resolves to nothing.                      |
| `presets_are_sane`                                 | Every preset is a `{ p50, p99 }` pair with 0 < p50 ≤ p99 ≤ 1 s, and hit ratios are in 0..=1.                                                          |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/replay/` — token bucket and slot gate

One test target (`cargo test -p kaanal-core --test replay`), one module per topic in `tests/replay/` (R3.8):

| Module           | What it covers                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `common.rs`      | Topology, hops, plans, the run loop.                           |
| `bucket_gate.rs` | The token bucket and the slot gate.                            |
| `engine.rs`      | FIFO hand-over, answers, stamps, clock, table, submit, drain.  |
| `limits.rs`      | Rate limits and queue overflow.                                |
| `spans.rs`       | Timeout spans, zombies, deadlines.                             |
| `reference.rs`   | The engine against a reference queue.                          |
| `cause.rs`       | The failure cause per failure kind.                            |
| `hold.rs`        | `hold: call`: held slots, failure, zombies, parallel, no leak. |

| Test                                                             | What it proves                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `bucket_with_burst_one_allows_one_then_asks_for_a_full_interval` | Burst 1, 10 ms: one take passes, the next at once asks for 10 ms, at 4 ms for 6 ms, and at 10 ms passes.                     |
| `bucket_starts_full_and_refills_one_token_per_interval`          | Burst 3 allows three at once and refuses the fourth with one interval; one interval later one token is back.                 |
| `bucket_never_banks_more_than_its_burst`                         | After an idle hour a burst-2 bucket still allows exactly two takes, then asks for one interval.                              |
| `refusing_does_not_spend_a_token`                                | Five refused takes do not push the next token away: it is available at the original time.                                    |
| `extreme_buckets_do_not_overflow`                                | `interval_ns = u64::MAX` with `burst = u32::MAX` at `now = u64::MAX`, and a 1 ns interval at 1 GHz, never overflow or panic. |
| `gate_runs_up_to_its_slots_then_queues_then_overflows`           | One slot and a queue of one: `Run`, `Queued`, `Overflow`; `busy`, `queued`, `slots` and `overflow_status` report it.         |
| `a_queue_of_zero_overflows_as_soon_as_every_slot_is_busy`        | Two slots and a queue of 0: two `Run`, then `Overflow` with nothing queued.                                                  |
| `release_hands_the_slot_to_the_oldest_waiter_without_freeing_it` | `release` returns waiters in FIFO order, `busy` stays 1 during hand-over, a later arrival queues behind them (no barging).   |
| `an_unbounded_queue_never_overflows`                             | With no queue cap, 9 999 waiters all queue.                                                                                  |
| `cancel_removes_one_waiter_and_keeps_the_order_of_the_rest`      | Cancelling the middle waiter leaves the others in order; cancelling a waiter that is not queued has no effect.               |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/dist.rs` — latency distributions

| Test                                            | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `lognormal_hits_p50_and_p99_within_5_percent`   | 100 000 samples of a fitted lognormal land within 5 % of the configured p50 and p99 (mirrors NFR-11's 5 % accuracy bar).                                                                                                                         |
| `fixed_is_fixed`                                | `Fixed(5ms)` always samples 5 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `p99_not_above_p50_falls_back_to_fixed`         | Degenerate p50/p99 pairs (equal, or p99 < p50) become `Fixed(p50)` instead of producing a broken distribution.                                                                                                                                   |
| `fat_tail_is_capped_at_ten_times_p99`           | Even a 1 ms/1 s distribution never samples above 10×p99 — a fat tail can't hang a request.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `same_seed_same_samples`                        | Same seed → identical sample vectors (determinism).                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `exponential_has_the_configured_mean_and_a_cap` | 100k samples: mean within 2%, median ≈ mean·ln 2, never above 10 × mean, same seed → same samples.                                                                                                                                               |
| `stable_hash_is_fixed_and_separates_parts`      | The FNV-1a hash matches hard-coded known values for the empty input and the non-empty `["a","bc"]` (so it can never silently change) and `["ab","c"]` ≠ `["a","bc"]`. The same vectors are in kaanal-data to pin the two copies together (R4.4). |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/topology.rs` — graph validation

| Test                                                      | What it proves                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `builds_base`                                             | Happy path: 3 nodes, gateway identified (`Option`), name lookups, edge lookups, `out_edges` order.                                                              |
| `admission_and_rate_limit_are_built_from_the_config`      | `Node.admission` comes from `concurrency` (number or map) or `pool`; `rate_limit` becomes an integer interval (4 rps = 250 ms) and a burst; unset stays `None`. |
| `fractional_rps_rounds_to_the_nearest_nanosecond`         | 3 rps gives 333 333 333 ns and 1e9 rps gives 1 ns.                                                                                                              |
| `rejects_cycle`                                           | `db → app` closing a loop fails with `Cycle(["app","db","app"])`.                                                                                               |
| `rejects_unknown_node_in_edge`                            | An edge to `redis` (undefined) fails naming the edge number and the name.                                                                                       |
| `second_gateway_is_rejected_by_name`                      | A second gateway fails with `SecondGateway` naming it (the first is kept).                                                                                      |
| `rejects_database_without_ops`                            | A database with no flavor needs `ops: read/write/scan`.                                                                                                         |
| `gateway_is_optional`                                     | Service → database with no gateway builds, as does a lone monolith with a flavor (inline ops); `gateway` is `None`.                                             |
| `rejects_service_spec_without_gateway_edge`               | A service with a spec unreachable from the gateway is an error (its routes would never be walked).                                                              |
| `services_may_call_services`                              | `service → service` is allowed (R1.24); the caller has two out-edges.                                                                                           |
| `errors_display_human_text`                               | Error `Display` is human prose (`cycle: a -> b -> a`).                                                                                                          |
| `rejects_duplicate_edge`                                  | The same from→to pair twice is `DuplicateEdge` with its edge number.                                                                                            |
| `a_code_built_config_with_a_bad_edge_prob_is_rejected`    | An edge `prob` of NaN, 0, -1, 2 or infinity set on a `Config` built in code is `BadProb` at that edge (the loader check does not run for it).                   |
| `rejects_edges_the_executor_would_ignore`                 | `gateway → database` is `ForbiddenEdge` naming the caller.                                                                                                      |
| `a_long_chain_of_services_does_not_overflow_the_stack`    | R6.12: 20 000 services in a line load and closing the chain reports a 20 001-name cycle, on a 256 KiB thread stack (a recursive check would overflow it).       |
| `cycle_path_starts_at_the_loop_not_the_dfs_root`          | `x → y → z → y` reports `y -> z -> y`, not a path from the DFS root.                                                                                            |
| `a_cycle_only_reachable_from_a_later_start_node_is_found` | A loop in a second component (`c ⇄ d`) is found after a clean chain.                                                                                            |
| `edge_table_matches_r1_24`                                | Every kind-to-kind pair is allowed or `ForbiddenEdge` exactly per the R1.24 table (the gateway and auth twins are skipped: one of each only).                   |
| `forbidden_edge_names_what_is_allowed`                    | The error text lists what the caller may call (`nothing` for leaves).                                                                                           |
| `a_topology_needs_somewhere_to_enter`                     | No gateway, service or monolith (empty, or only a database) is `NoEntry`; a lone gateway is fine.                                                               |
| `self_and_duplicate_edges_are_rejected`                   | `SelfLoop` and `DuplicateEdge`, each with its edge number.                                                                                                      |
| `cycle_through_auth_is_rejected`                          | A cycle through an `auth` node is reported as `svc -> auth -> svc`.                                                                                             |
| `monolith_works_both_ways`                                | Inline ops, database edges and both are accepted; partial `ops` without a flavor is `MissingOps`.                                                               |
| `cache_costs_come_from_the_flavor_or_the_user`            | Cache hit/miss come from the flavor preset unless the user gives them; a cache with neither is `MissingCache`.                                                  |
| `edge_call_ratio_is_carried`                              | `calls: 3` is carried onto the edge; the default is 1.                                                                                                          |
| `second_auth_is_rejected_by_name`                         | A second auth node fails with `SecondAuth` naming it, placed at `nodes.<it>` (5:3), with the "at most one auth node" text.                                      |
| `an_auth_only_topology_is_valid`                          | A lone auth node builds (`gateway` None, `auth` Some(0)); `auth` is None without one; `NoEntry` has the new message that names the auth node.                   |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/exec/` — the chain executor

One test target (`cargo test -p kaanal-core --test exec`), one module per topic in `tests/exec/` (R3.8):

| Module             | What it covers                                                                           |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `common.rs`        | Base config and run functions.                                                           |
| `basic.rs`         | The walk: determinism, failures, latency, gatewayless.                                   |
| `timeouts.rs`      | Spans, hang, reset, malformed, deadlines.                                                |
| `endpoint.rs`      | Endpoint and node-at-endpoint chaos, cache timeouts.                                     |
| `deadlines.rs`     | Ties, earliest deadline, parallel branches, repeats.                                     |
| `layers.rs`        | Stacked layers and banded failures.                                                      |
| `prob.rs`          | Branch probability: one draw per step, skipped calls leave nothing.                      |
| `balance.rs`       | The pure replica pick: round-robin, random, weighted, down replicas.                     |
| `replicas.rs`      | A call binds one replica of its callee for the whole subtree.                            |
| `links.rs`         | A bandwidth-limited edge is a FIFO single-slot resource of its own.                      |
| `replica_chaos.rs` | Chaos on one replica: down replicas skipped, latency and 503.                            |
| `links.rs`         | Payload cost: link hops from bandwidth and bytes, `per_kb` on the proc hop.              |
| `link_chaos.rs`    | Chaos on a link: partition, drops, jitter, extra latency, a bandwidth cap, a cache edge. |

| Test                                                                  | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `same_inputs_same_outcome`                                            | Same `(seed, endpoint, n)` → identical outcome (determinism, rule R4.1).                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `different_n_gives_different_timings`                                 | The request counter changes the draws.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `happy_path_visits_gateway_service_db_in_order`                       | Exact hop sequence `nginx.proc → net → app.proc → net → db.read`, result `Ok`, and `total()` equals the hop sum.                                                                                                                                       |
| `error_rate_one_always_fails_at_that_node`                            | 100 requests all fail at `db` with 504; the 4 hops before it still count toward the delay.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `execute_never_sets_retry_after`                                      | After a chaos failure `result` is `Err { node, status, retry_after: None }`: only the replay engine sets `retry_after`.                                                                                                                                |
| `down_node_returns_503`                                               | `down` on `app` → 503 at `app`, walk stops after 2 hops.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `error_rate_zero_never_fails`                                         | 1000 requests with a zero-rate chaos entry all succeed.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `latency_add_lands_on_that_node`                                      | +100 ms chaos appears in the db op hop's duration.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `inert_chaos_entry_does_not_change_timings`                           | An all-default chaos entry leaves the outcome bit-identical — the error roll is always drawn, so chaos settings never shift the timing RNG.                                                                                                            |
| `static_route_only_touches_gateway`                                   | `entry: None` walks only the gateway proc hop.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `op_inference`                                                        | `GET /users/{id}` → read, `GET /users` → scan, POST/DELETE → write; case-insensitive; `Op::name()`.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `gateway_down_fails_static_route_with_no_hops`                        | Gateway chaos applies even to static routes, failing before any hop is recorded.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `chaos_validate_enforces_the_shared_limits`                           | R6.9: `NodeChaos::validate` accepts defaults and the exact caps (5 min, rate 1.0, status 500/504) and rejects 300 001 ms, rate 1.5 / -0.1, status 418 / 505; `MAX_LATENCY_ADD` is 300 s.                                                               |
| `gatewayless_walk_starts_at_the_owner`                                | No gateway: hops are `app.proc`, `db` net, `db` read; no gateway hop, no gateway net hop; total 4 ms.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `callees_below_the_entry_are_charged`                                 | The default chain charges a monolith (proc and inline op), an external API and a cache lookup: hop kinds in edge order, preset costs above zero.                                                                                                       |
| `a_node_without_timeout_makes_no_spans`                               | No `timeout:` anywhere: the plan has no `Span` item, so configs without the field plan exactly as before (B2a criterion 7).                                                                                                                            |
| `timeout_on_the_callee_wraps_edge_hop_and_body_in_a_span`             | A `timeout:` on a callee puts one `Span` before the edge hop whose `size` covers the edge hop and the callee body.                                                                                                                                     |
| `execute_reports_a_zero_load_timeout_at_the_deadline`                 | A callee slower than its `timeout` at zero load: `execute` answers 504, kind `timeout`, failing node = the span node, hops clipped at the deadline.                                                                                                    |
| `gateway_timeout_covers_the_whole_chain`                              | A gateway `timeout` shorter than the chain behind it fires at the gateway (Z9), whatever node is slow.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `a_planned_fail_earlier_than_the_deadline_wins`                       | A chaos `Fail` before the deadline decides the result; the deadline never replaces an earlier failure.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `reset_and_malformed_are_planned_fails_with_their_kind`               | `error_kind: reset` / `malformed` plan a `Fail` at the hop start carrying that `FailKind`; no extra RNG draw (Z15).                                                                                                                                    |
| `hang_is_a_capped_hop_then_a_timeout_fail`                            | Chaos `hang` with no timeout anywhere: one own hop of `HANG_CAP` (60 s) then a 504 `Fail` of kind `timeout` at its end (Z6).                                                                                                                           |
| `hang_uses_the_node_timeout_when_set`                                 | The hang hop lasts the node's `timeout` instead of 60 s.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `endpoint_chaos_failure_belongs_to_the_entry_node_at_offset_zero`     | Endpoint-wide chaos fails at offset 0 and the failing node is the entry node (gateway, else owner) (Z10).                                                                                                                                              |
| `endpoint_latency_is_a_delay_hop_on_the_entry_node`                   | Endpoint `latency_add` is a leading `Delay` hop on the entry node (Z18).                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `node_at_chaos_only_hits_its_endpoint`                                | Chaos on `node @ endpoint` fires for that endpoint and leaves other endpoints through the same node alone.                                                                                                                                             |
| `cache_timeout_is_a_capped_miss`                                      | A cache lookup slower than the cache's `timeout` is a miss charged exactly the timeout; the request goes on (Z17).                                                                                                                                     |
| `hang_on_a_cache_is_a_miss_charged_the_cap`                           | Chaos `hang` on a cache is a miss charged the timeout or `HANG_CAP`; a cache never fails the request.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `execute_ignores_limits_with_spans`                                   | `execute` stays the independent zero-load oracle: pools and rate limits are ignored even when spans are present (Z13).                                                                                                                                 |
| `hang_plans_a_capped_own_hop_then_a_timeout_fail`                     | Same hang plan shape seen through `sample_plan`: capped hop, then `Fail { kind: Timeout, status: 504 }`.                                                                                                                                               |
| `a_planned_fail_at_the_deadline_instant_wins_the_tie`                 | A planned `Fail` at exactly the span's deadline instant wins (Z14).                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `the_earliest_deadline_wins_not_the_first_span_in_plan_order`         | With nested or sibling spans, the earliest deadline instant decides, not the first span in plan order.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `a_span_in_a_parallel_branch_cuts_every_hop_at_its_deadline`          | A deadline inside a `parallel` branch clips every hop of the request at that instant, siblings included.                                                                                                                                               |
| `repeat_makes_one_span_per_repetition`                                | A `repeat` call gets one span per repetition, each with its own deadline.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `endpoint_chaos_down_reset_and_malformed_fail_at_offset_zero`         | Endpoint chaos `down`, `reset` and `malformed` each plan a `Fail` at offset 0 with the right kind.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `endpoint_chaos_hang_is_a_delay_hop_of_the_cap_then_a_504_at_the_cap` | Endpoint chaos `hang` is a `Delay` hop of the cap, then a 504 at the cap.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `a_banded_mix_fails_with_each_kind_at_its_share`                      | Through `execute`: 30% `reset` (502) over 30% 503 over 2000 requests gives about 30% resets and 21% 503s, nothing else (S8).                                                                                                                           |
| `a_stack_is_deterministic_and_a_single_layer_ignores_the_stack`       | The same two-layer stack compiled twice gives identical outcomes for 500 requests; for each failure kind a 50% layer alone and the same layer wrapped in empty layers give identical outcomes request by request, and between 150 and 350 of 500 fail. |
| `stacked_latency_adds_up_and_clamps`                                  | 10 s + 20 s of stacked node latency is 30 s; 200 s + 200 s clamps to `MAX_LATENCY_ADD` (S9).                                                                                                                                                           |
| `prob_one_changes_no_plan`                                            | (`prob.rs`) `prob: 1` on a step and on the edge plans exactly as no `prob`, for 200 requests: no draw is made.                                                                                                                                         |
| `prob_half_takes_about_half`                                          | `prob: 0.5` takes the call in 45 to 55% of 4000 requests.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `an_edge_prob_is_the_default_and_a_step_prob_wins`                    | An edge `prob` of 0.25 is the default of a call over it (20 to 30%); a step's `prob: 1` overrides it (always taken).                                                                                                                                   |
| `one_draw_covers_all_repeats`                                         | `repeat: 3, prob: 0.5` gives 0 or 3 network hops to the callee, never 1 or 2.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `a_skipped_call_leaves_nothing_and_shifts_no_other_draw`              | When the first of two calls to `db` is skipped there is one `db` span and the surviving call's durations equal the same request with `prob: 1` (occurrences do not shift); when it is taken the plan equals the `prob: 1` plan.                        |
| `a_prob_call_in_a_parallel_branch_keeps_the_plan_consistent`          | A skipped call inside a `parallel` branch leaves the `Fork` and `Branch` sizes consistent with the items, and over 300 requests the branch is both skipped (one database call) and taken (two).                                                        |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/visits.rs` — expected visits per node

| Test                                                                     | What it proves                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `a_plain_chain_counts_one_per_call`                                      | Gateway, service and database each 1; an unused cache 0.                                                                                            |
| `calls_3_and_prob_multiply`                                              | An edge with `calls: 3, prob: 0.5` gives the database 1.5 visits.                                                                                   |
| `a_cache_hit_scales_later_steps`                                         | A cache with hit ratio 0.8 is visited once and the database after it 0.2.                                                                           |
| `repeated_lookups_stop_at_the_first_hit`                                 | Two lookups visit the cache 1.2 times and let 0.04 through.                                                                                         |
| `default_cache_lookups_skip_writes`                                      | The default chain's cache lookup counts for a `GET` and not for a `POST`; the database sees 0.2 and 1.                                              |
| `parallel_branches_add`                                                  | Two database calls in one `parallel` block count 2.                                                                                                 |
| `a_hit_in_one_branch_does_not_scale_the_other_or_what_follows`           | A hit inside one branch scales only that branch (0.2 + 1 + 1 = 2.2).                                                                                |
| `the_entry_node_counts_once_without_a_gateway`                           | With no gateway the owning service is the entry node: 1.                                                                                            |
| `an_auth_node_validating_locally_is_visited_inline`                      | With no gateway-to-auth edge, `POST /login` visits the gateway and the auth node once each (the auth `proc` sits inline in the gateway's sequence). |
| `an_auth_request_edge_visits_the_auth_node_once`                         | With the edge, auth is visited once by `POST /login` and once by an ordinary route.                                                                 |
| `round_robin_cycles_through_live_replicas`                               | (`balance.rs`) Three live replicas are taken 0, 1, 2, 0, 1, 2, 0 for requests 0 to 6.                                                               |
| `a_down_replica_is_skipped_and_the_cycle_closes_over_the_rest`           | With replica 1 down the cycle is 0, 2, 0, 2, 0, 2.                                                                                                  |
| `the_offset_moves_where_the_cycle_starts`                                | Offsets 0, 1, 2, 3 start the cycle at 0, 1, 2, 0.                                                                                                   |
| `all_down_gives_none_for_every_policy`                                   | Round-robin, random and weighted return `None` when no replica is live.                                                                             |
| `random_is_uniform_within_5_percent_over_20000`                          | Four replicas each take 25% of 20 000 draws, within 5% of that share.                                                                               |
| `random_never_picks_a_down_replica_and_spreads_over_the_rest`            | A down replica gets 0; the other three take a third each within 5%.                                                                                 |
| `weighted_3_1_1_follows_the_weights_within_5_percent`                    | Shares 0.6, 0.2, 0.2 within 5% of each.                                                                                                             |
| `a_zero_weight_gets_nothing_and_a_down_replica_renormalises`             | A 0 weight is never picked; with the heaviest replica down the others split 50/50.                                                                  |
| `when_only_zero_weight_replicas_are_live_the_pick_falls_back_to_uniform` | `[5, 0, 0]` with replica 0 down picks the other two evenly.                                                                                         |
| `same_inputs_same_pick`                                                  | Random and weighted give the same replica for the same inputs.                                                                                      |
| `a_different_node_name_draws_a_different_stream`                         | The node name is part of the draw: two nodes get different picks.                                                                                   |

| `replicas_1_changes_no_plan_and_every_hop_is_replica_0` | (`replicas.rs`) `replicas: 1` (with a `balance`) plans exactly as no replicas for 100 requests, and every hop is replica 0. |
| `a_call_binds_its_whole_subtree_to_one_replica` | With `app` on 3 random replicas, the net hop into app and app's own work share a replica for 200 requests, and every replica is reached. |
| `two_calls_to_one_node_advance_round_robin` | `[ db, db ]` on 3 replicas: the second call takes the next replica, and the next request starts one further on. |
| `the_replica_never_changes_service_times` | Hop nodes, kinds, starts and durations are identical for 1 replica and for 3 and 4 random replicas on the same seed. |
| `an_inline_auth_node_never_inherits_the_gateways_replica` | (`replicas.rs`) Gateway on 3 replicas, auth on 1, no gateway-to-auth edge: every auth hop of `POST /login` is replica 0 for 60 requests. |
| `an_inline_auth_node_with_replicas_is_balanced_on_its_own` | Gateway on 1 replica, auth on 3 random: auth's work reaches all three replicas, each below 3. |
| `a_down_replicated_cache_is_a_miss_not_a_failure` | A cache on 2 replicas with node-level `down` serves a request that succeeds with a `CacheMiss` hop (Z17). |
| `no_bandwidth_means_no_link_hops` | (`links.rs`) Bytes without a `bandwidth` on the edge plan no `Link` hop. | | `zero_bytes_means_no_link_hops` | (`links.rs`) A bandwidth with 0 bytes plans no `Link` hop. | | `a_link_hop_lasts_bytes_over_bandwidth` | 10 000 bytes over 1 MB/s is 10 ms, on the edge target, kind `Link { edge, resp: false }`. | | `request_and_response_each_get_a_hop` | Request link, then net, then the callee body, then the response link, each starting where the last ended. | | `transfer_is_capped_at_10_hours` | 1 GB over 1 B/s is capped at 10 h, not overflowed. | | `per_kb_adds_to_proc_once_per_body` | `per_kb: 1ms` with 3 KB + 1 KB adds 4 ms to the one `proc` hop. |
| `per_kb_on_a_database_adds_to_its_op_hop` | A database with `per_kb: 1ms` and 2 KB of payload adds 2 ms to its op hop. |
| `a_cache_edge_has_link_hops_and_a_miss_has_no_response_transfer` | A bandwidth edge to a cache: a hit has a request and a response link hop, a miss only the request one. |
| `partition_waits_the_callee_timeout_then_504` | (`link_chaos.rs`) A `down` edge: a `Net` hop of the callee's timeout (200 ms; 60 s without one), then a 504 timeout. |
| `link_drop_is_a_502_reset` | `error_rate: 1` on an edge fails at the call with a 502 reset, no net hop to the callee; kind `hang` waits and gives 504. |
| `a_drop_rate_hits_about_its_share` | At 0.3, 500 to 700 of 2000 requests are dropped. |
| `jitter_is_bounded_and_deterministic` | 5 ms of jitter: the same request jitters the same, the other edge never moves, the hop stays within 1 ms plus 5 ms, it varies and can go below the base. |
| `latency_add_extends_the_net_hop` | +20 ms on an edge makes its net hop 21 ms. |
| `a_bandwidth_cap_lengthens_the_transfer` | 1 MB/s with 10 KB takes 10 ms; capped at 500 kB/s 20 ms; a cap above the bandwidth changes nothing. |
| `link_chaos_off_changes_no_plan` | An inert edge entry and chaos on an edge no call crosses plan exactly like no chaos. |
| `a_dropped_link_to_a_cache_is_a_miss_not_a_failure` | A partitioned cache edge is a miss charged 60 s and the request still succeeds. |
| `an_entry_node_with_replicas_picks_without_a_calling_edge` | A gateway with 3 round-robin replicas takes consecutive replicas for consecutive requests. |
| `an_edge_balance_overrides_the_node` | A node on `random` with an edge `weighted: [0, 0, 1]` always takes replica 2. |
| `a_replica_down_is_skipped_by_every_policy` | (`replica_chaos.rs`) With replica 1 of 3 down, round-robin, random and weighted never put a hop on it over 300 requests and still reach 0 and 2. |
| `every_replica_down_fails_with_503` | All three replicas down: a 503 `Status` failure at `db`, and no hop on `db` (the call never starts). |
| `a_node_down_takes_every_replica_down_with_it` | A node-level `down` with 2 replicas fails the call with a 503. |
| `replica_latency_chaos_hits_only_that_replica` | 30 ms on replica 2 lengthens only the hops on replica 2 by exactly 30 ms, and moves no pick. |
| `a_replica_entry_inherits_the_node_entry` | Node latency 10 ms plus replica 0 latency 5 ms: replica 0 gets +15 ms, replica 1 +10 ms. |
| `a_replica_entry_also_folds_the_node_at_endpoint_entry` | Node-at-endpoint 7 ms plus replica 1 3 ms: replica 1 gets +10 ms through that endpoint, replica 0 +7 ms. |
| `a_replica_layer_can_be_read_listed_and_removed` | `ChaosState` stores, reads, counts, lists, compiles and removes a `Replica` key. |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/semantics.rs` — the A2 decisions as timelines

| Test                                                        | What it proves                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `gateway_chain_runs_proc_then_auth_then_the_entry`          | E11: gateway `proc`, then its auth call, then the call into the entry; hand-computed starts and a total of 18 ms.                                |
| `default_chain_inherits_the_write_op_and_skips_the_cache`   | E10: a write endpoint's default chain skips the cache and runs `db.write`.                                                                       |
| `step_op_overrides_for_that_step_only`                      | `op:` on a step applies to that step; the next step keeps the inherited op.                                                                      |
| `via_uses_the_target_endpoints_own_op`                      | `via:` runs the target's op and body (`POST /orders`: no cache, `db.write`); the same call without `via` inherits the read.                      |
| `error_map_belongs_to_the_served_endpoint`                  | E12: the served endpoint's map decides; a `via` target's map is not used (and warns).                                                            |
| `cache_hit_ends_only_the_enclosing_sequence`                | E9: a hit inside a callee's body ends that body; at the top of the chain it ends the endpoint chain.                                             |
| `cache_hit_in_a_repeated_call_ends_only_that_repetition`    | A hit ends one repetition of a repeated call, not the repetitions after it.                                                                      |
| `cache_hit_in_a_parallel_branch_ends_only_that_branch`      | A hit in one `parallel` branch stops that branch only: the other branch still runs its db read and the block waits for it.                       |
| `failing_cache_is_a_miss_not_a_failure`                     | E9/D12: a down or erroring cache is a miss (plus `latency_add`) and the request succeeds.                                                        |
| `parallel_ends_with_its_slowest_branch`                     | E14: branches start together, the block ends with the slowest; the plan shape (`F2/10 B2 … B6 …`).                                               |
| `parallel_fails_at_the_earliest_failure_and_cuts_siblings`  | The earliest failure wins even from a higher branch; running siblings are cut, later ones dropped; `kept`, `fail` and `cancelled_by`.            |
| `parallel_tie_goes_to_the_lowest_branch`                    | Equal failure instants go to the lowest branch index, in either branch order.                                                                    |
| `repeat_runs_in_sequence_with_independent_draws`            | `repeat: 3` runs back to back with three different draws.                                                                                        |
| `inserting_a_step_keeps_other_steps_draws`                  | R1.34: adding a step with another target leaves the `db` and `app` draws bit-identical.                                                          |
| `parallel_branch_order_does_not_change_draws`               | Swapping `parallel` branches with different targets changes no draw and not the total.                                                           |
| `draws_follow_the_r1_34_formula`                            | Each hop's duration equals a fresh RNG seeded `stable_hash(path, node, tag)` with the D8 path, chaos roll first for own hops.                    |
| `error_rate_holds_across_seeds`                             | A 30% chaos rate holds over 2 000 seeds (realistic mode only changes the seed).                                                                  |
| `diamond_calls_to_a_shared_body_draw_independently`         | Two `orders` calls each with an inner `db` call draw different durations: the parent path is chained into the seed (Task 2 review, C4).          |
| `an_edge_with_calls_looks_the_cache_up_until_a_hit`         | An edge with `calls: 3` to a cache: three lookups in sequence on a miss, one on a hit (which ends app's body, so no database), none for a write. |
| `cache_repetitions_draw_from_their_own_paths`               | Each cache lookup repetition draws from its own path (D8 with the repetition index): three different, formula-exact `c.net` durations.           |
| `timeout_loads_on_every_node_kind_and_reaches_the_topology` | `timeout:` parses on all seven node kinds and arrives as `Node::timeout` in the topology.                                                        |
| `timeout_range_is_checked_with_a_positioned_error`          | `timeout: 0s` and `timeout: 6m` fail at load with the error placed at the value.                                                                 |
| `timeout_boundaries_load_on_gateway_service_and_cache`      | 1 ns and exactly 5 min are accepted.                                                                                                             |

## `crates/kaanal-core/tests/chain/` — endpoint compile

One test target (`cargo test -p kaanal-core --test chain`), one module per topic in `tests/chain/` (R3.8):

| Module           | What it covers                                           |
