| `additional_properties_schema_adds_up_to_three_keys_and_none_when_absent` | With an `additionalProperties` schema 0 to 3 extra keys of that type appear (the count varies across seeds); without it or with `false` none do; declared fields are unchanged by adding the keyword.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `int32_bounds_are_honoured`                                               | `format: int32` with a lone huge `minimum` or `maximum` stays inside the `i32` range.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `password_and_byte_respect_lengths`                                       | A `password` with `minLength: 20` or `maxLength: 5` is fitted (characters).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `typeless_additional_properties_is_an_object`                             | A schema with only `additionalProperties: { type: integer }` is treated as an object.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `one_of_picks_different_branches_across_seeds_and_is_stable_per_seed`     | `oneOf` and `anyOf` over three branch types reach all three across 60 seeds and repeat per seed; empty `oneOf`/`anyOf` arrays are skipped without a panic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `adding_a_sibling_property_next_to_one_of_changes_nothing_else`           | R4.4 with `oneOf`: adding five properties (one itself a `oneOf`, one nesting the same `pet`, one with `examples`, one with `additionalProperties`) leaves every other value and every branch pick identical over 100 seeds; all three branches are reached, and two identical sibling `oneOf`s do not always pick the same branch (the pick follows each field's own seed). |
| `discriminator_with_mapping_sets_the_tag`                                 | A `mapping` (full ref and bare name) sets `petType` to `dog` or `cat` with the matching branch's properties; no warnings.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `discriminator_without_mapping_uses_a_single_value_enum`                  | Tags come from a single-value `enum` or a `const` on the property; no warnings.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `discriminator_with_neither_warns_at_startup_and_still_generates`         | No mapping, enum or const: one warning per untagged branch (not "not supported"), and the tag is still generated as a string.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `generated_discriminated_object_validates_against_its_schema`             | Two branches that differ only by the tag, via mapping and via `enum`/`const`, validate against the original schema (`oneOf` exclusivity) over 60 seeds, both tags appearing; pins the mapping rewrite.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `discriminator_mapping_to_no_branch_warns`                                | A mapping value naming no branch warns "mapping 'cat' names no oneOf/anyOf branch" plus the untagged-branch warning, two in all.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `a_reused_discriminated_schema_warns_once_per_branch`                     | The same discriminated schema under three properties (one in an array) warns twice, once per branch, not per use.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `nested_and_deep_one_of_stays_within_the_depth_budget`                    | `oneOf` nested 40 deep does not panic; 20 single-branch levels give `null` and `ApiModel::warnings()` says "deeper than 16".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/pack.rs` — locale packs

The pack format and the two built-in packs ([source](files/kaanal-data/pack.md)).

| Test                                                          | What it proves                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `en_in_builtin_parses_and_has_required_blocks`                | `en-IN` is built in, has `INR`, `+91`, India, at least 60 places each with a state, and is what `Options::default()` uses.                                          |
| `pack_without_streets_is_an_error_naming_the_block`           | A missing or empty `streets` fails and names the block (with a line when empty).                                                                                    |
| `non_ascii_latin_form_is_an_error_with_line_and_column`       | `latin: Jörg` fails at its own line and column (12:12), saying "ASCII", with no duplicated "at line".                                                               |
| `state_in_template_with_a_stateless_place_names_the_place`    | An address with `{state}` and a place without a state fails naming the place.                                                                                       |
| `stateless_pack_without_state_in_template_loads`              | A pack with no states and no `{state}` loads.                                                                                                                       |
| `unknown_builtin_tag_is_none`, `every_builtin_tag_resolves`   | `Pack::builtin` is `None` for an unknown tag and returns a pack with the right tag for every built-in one.                                                          |
| `en_in_matches_the_lists_it_replaced`                         | The built-in `en-IN` pack starts with the pre-D1 `words.rs` first names, surnames, streets and cities, in order, with no `latin:` forms.                            |
| `unknown_address_placeholder_is_an_error`                     | An address template with an unknown placeholder (`{house}`) fails at `address:`.                                                                                    |
| `bad_digit_patterns_are_errors`                               | Phone and postcode patterns with a bad `{n}` (not a number, signed, 0, above 20, unclosed) fail naming `{n}`.                                                       |
| `unsupported_version_is_an_error`                             | `version: 2` fails at the value.                                                                                                                                    |
| `unknown_fields_are_errors_with_position`                     | An unknown key at the top level, in `currency`, in a place or in a name fails with "unknown field" at its line.                                                     |
| `empty_names_and_latin_forms_are_errors_with_position`        | An empty name, `name:` or `latin:` and an empty surname fail with "empty" at their line and column.                                                                 |
| `a_leading_bom_is_accepted`                                   | A UTF-8 byte order mark does not break parsing.                                                                                                                     |
| `ascii_local_prefers_latin_then_folds_diacritics_then_user_n` | `latin:` wins; `José` gives `jose`, `Straße` gives `strasse`; a CJK name with nothing foldable gives `user<n>`.                                                     |
| `ascii_local_keeps_only_lowercase_letters_and_digits`         | Spaces, apostrophes and hyphens are dropped.                                                                                                                        |
| `ascii_local_never_panics_and_is_always_ascii`                | Emoji, combining marks, RTL text and a sweep of code points never panic and always give lower-case ASCII.                                                           |
| `en_us_person_has_usd_united_states_plus_one`                 | `en-US` gives `USD`, `$`, United States, `+1`, a state-bearing address template, and objects built with it show them.                                               |
| `builtin_packs_meet_the_medium_size_floor`                    | Each built-in pack has at least 200 first names, 200 surnames, 60 places and 20 streets (D1.7).                                                                     |
| `builtin_lists_have_no_duplicates`                            | No repeated name, street or city in a built-in pack.                                                                                                                |
| `builtin_packs_are_ascii_clean_for_latin_forms`               | Every built-in name has a non-empty lower-case ASCII email form.                                                                                                    |
| `builtin_tags_lists_all_seven`                                | `builtin_tags()` is exactly `en-IN`, `en-US`, `en-GB`, `de-DE`, `fr-FR`, `es-ES`, `pt-BR`.                                                                          |
| `starter_packs_parse_and_meet_the_size_floor`                 | Each starter pack has its country, currency and phone prefix, at least 150 first and last names, 50 places and a `geo` block, and objects built with it carry them. |
| `de_de_is_stateless_and_formats_its_address`                  | `de-DE` has no state in any place and no `{state}` in its template; generated addresses end `, <zip> <city>`.                                                       |
| `every_starter_email_is_ascii_example_com`                    | Over 100 seeds per built-in pack, emails are ASCII and end `@example.com`.                                                                                          |
| `starter_postcodes_have_the_national_shape`                   | `de-DE`, `fr-FR`, `es-ES` postcodes expand to 5 digits and `pt-BR` to 8 (`00000-000`).                                                                              |
| `builtin_postcodes_look_valid_for_the_country`                | `en-IN` postcodes are 6 digits not starting with 0, `en-US` 5 digits.                                                                                               |
| `builtin_files_are_marked_synthetic`                          | Every built-in file starts with `# synthetic data, authored for Kaanal` (FR-09, R5.7).                                                                              |
| `coordinates_follow_the_pack_geo_block_or_the_whole_globe`    | `latitude`/`longitude` stay in `en-IN`'s and `en-US`'s `geo` bounds; a pack without `geo:` spans both hemispheres within -90..90 and -180..180.                     |
| `geo_bounds_are_checked_at_the_value`                         | `geo` parses; a min above its max, a value out of range or NaN fails at the `geo.lat`/`geo.lon` value, and a missing `lon` fails with a line.                       |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/dictionary.rs` — the field dictionary

Parsing, matching and fit rules of `data.yaml` and the built-in dictionary ([source](files/kaanal-data/dictionary.md)).

| Test                                                               | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `normalisation_makes_camel_snake_kebab_share_a_key`                | `userEmail`, `user_email` and `User-Email` hit one entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `exact_key_beats_suffix_and_longer_suffix_beats_shorter`           | `parentOrderId` takes its exact key; `lastOrderId` takes `*order_id` over `*id`; a suffix matches whole words, so `paid` and `valid` are not ids.                                                                                                            |
| `unknown_generator_error_lists_valid_ones_with_line`               | `gen: sku_code` fails with a line and column and lists the valid generators.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `project_entry_beats_format_but_not_enum_or_example`               | D1.8: a project entry beats `format: email`; an `enum` or `example` beats the project entry.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `format_beats_the_built_in_name_entry`                             | `format: uuid` on a field called `email` gives a uuid.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `misfit_entry_is_skipped_with_a_warning_naming_the_field`          | A `pick` of strings on an integer field is skipped (values stay in bounds) and `dictionary_warnings` names `GET /x`, the field and the type; built-ins and fitting entries are silent.                                                                       |
| `misfit_warnings_follow_what_the_generator_visits`                 | No misfit warning for a field with non-empty `examples` (list or map) or a non-empty `enum`; an empty one still warns. Only the first non-empty `oneOf`/`anyOf` is walked.                                                                                   |
| `money_clamps_to_schema_bounds`                                    | A project `money` entry clamps to the field's `minimum`/`maximum` for numbers and integers, and uses its own range when the field has none.                                                                                                                  |
| `text_trims_to_max_length_in_characters`                           | A generated `text` fits `maxLength` by characters; without bounds a paragraph is long.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `pick_template_static_values_pass_through`                         | `pick` values, `{digits:n}`/`{letters:n}` templates and `static` values come out as the file says (R5.7).                                                                                                                                                    |
| `user_strings_out_of_length_bounds_are_emitted_whole_and_warned`   | R1.56: a project `static` longer than `maxLength` (CJK) or shorter than `minLength`, a `pick` and a `template` longer than `maxLength` come out whole; `dictionary_warnings` names each of the four fields, and a template that meets its lengths is silent. |
| `generated_and_built_in_strings_still_fit_their_lengths`           | R1.56: a built-in `firstName` is cut to `maxLength`; a built-in `sku` template and `role` pick that cannot meet the lengths are skipped for a fitted word, never cut or padded; `phone` and `email` ignore lengths (R4.5); built-ins never warn.             |
| `bare_total_is_a_count_and_star_total_is_money`                    | R1.56: `total` is a whole number from 0 to 1000 on `integer` and `number` fields; `orderTotal`, `grandTotal`, `subtotal` and `cartSubtotal` on `number` fields are money (99 to 4999, with cents).                                                           |
| `builtin_dictionary_has_at_least_150_keys_and_covers_todays_names` | At least 150 keys, and every name the old heuristic table handled still gets a value.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `adding_a_sibling_property_leaves_other_values_identical`          | R4.4 with dictionary-driven fields.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `keys_that_normalise_alike_resolve_in_file_order_and_are_reported` | `user_email` and `userEmail` in one file: the later wins and `warnings()` lists the collision (R1.50d).                                                                                                                                                      |
| `check_errors_carry_a_position`                                    | Bad template placeholders, `min` above `max`, a non-finite bound, an empty suffix key and `version: 2` fail with a line and column.                                                                                                                          |
| `integer_entry_fits_number_fields`                                 | An `integer` entry fills a `number` field within its range, and gives a float inside bounds no whole number fits.                                                                                                                                            |
| `write_only_fields_are_not_warned`                                 | A misfit on a `writeOnly` property is never generated, so it is never warned.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `non_postal_addresses_and_states_are_not_places`                   | `ipAddress` is an IPv4 documentation address, `macAddress` a locally administered MAC, `walletAddress` is no place, `shippingAddress` ends in the object's city, `orderState` and `connectionState` are not regions.                                         |
| `integer_fields_never_get_floats_or_out_of_range_values`           | A `pick` of `[2.0, 3]` gives 3 for an integer field, and a `u64::MAX` `static` never lands in one.                                                                                                                                                           |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/selfcheck.rs` — self-validation (D2)

Generated responses checked against their schema ([source](files/kaanal-data/selfcheck.md)).

| Test                                                               | What it proves                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `valid_schema_has_no_findings`                                     | A plain object with bounded integer, string and array has no findings over 5 samples.                           |
| `reports_violation_with_path_and_keyword`                          | A pinned `example` over `maximum` is reported once per sample at `/total` with keyword `maximum`.               |
| `draft4_boolean_exclusive_minimum_is_accepted`                     | The OpenAPI 3.0 boolean `exclusiveMinimum` builds and validates.                                                |
| `required_empty_array_does_not_fail_build`                         | `required: []`, which Draft 4 rejects, is dropped before building.                                              |
| `format_violation_is_reported`                                     | A pinned non-email under `format: email` is reported (format checking is on).                                   |
| `write_only_property_is_not_required`                              | A `writeOnly` property that is also `required` is not a finding.                                                |
| `nullable_accepts_null_type`                                       | `nullable: true` accepts `null`.                                                                                |
| `budget_null_is_classified_not_violation`                          | Values cut by the body budget (and the `minItems` miss they cause) have `budget: true`.                         |
| `endpoint_without_schema_has_no_findings`                          | No response schema, no findings.                                                                                |
| `lines_are_deduplicated_and_worded_per_spec`                       | One line per endpoint, `GET /x: 5 of 5 generated samples violate the response schema: /items/*/total: maximum`. |
| `paged_route_is_checked_as_the_client_sees_it`                     | A paged envelope route generates through `generate_page` and has no findings.                                   |
| `unsupported_pattern_is_not_reported_again`                        | A lookahead `pattern` (refused by the sampler, warned at startup) is not reported as a violation.               |
| `ignored_keywords_are_not_reported_again`                          | `not` (still ignored) is removed from the validator's copy.                                                     |
| `a_pinned_value_that_breaks_multiple_of_is_reported`               | An `example` of 7 under `multipleOf: 5` is a finding (the spec contradicts itself).                             |
| `a_multiple_of_that_cannot_fit_is_left_to_its_own_warning`         | `multipleOf: 10` between 1 and 9 is not reported again by the self-check.                                       |
| `a_paged_route_is_not_blamed_for_min_items_beyond_one_page`        | `minItems: 45` on a paged array is not a finding (a page holds one page size).                                  |
| `a_pattern_that_cannot_meet_its_length_is_left_to_its_own_warning` | `^[A-Z]{8}$` with `maxLength: 4` is not reported again.                                                         |
| `unique_items_on_objects_is_left_to_its_own_warning`               | `uniqueItems` on an array of objects (unsupported) is not reported again.                                       |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/repo_specs.rs` — bundled specs generate valid data (D2)

Invalid output is a bug (FR-DATA-05) ([source](files/kaanal-data/selfcheck.md)).

| Test                                     | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `bundled_specs_generate_valid_responses` | The demo spec, the CLI and server fixtures, the examples and (when present locally) the petstore acceptance spec: 256 seeds per endpoint, no schema violation (body-budget cuts excepted). |

## `crates/kaanal-data/src/generate/pattern_tests.rs` (unit) — the pattern sampler (D2)

Strings sampled from a regex ([source](files/kaanal-data/generate.md#patternrs-d2-task-4)).

| Test                                                     | What it proves                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `sample_matches_every_supported_construct`               | Seven patterns (classes, alternation, optional, repeats, groups, `(?i)`, dot) match over 500 seeds each. |
| `classes_are_ascii`                                      | `\w`, `\d`, `[[:alpha:]]`, `\S` only ever draw ASCII.                                                    |
| `dot_and_negated_class_give_printable_characters`        | `.` and `[^a]` give printable ASCII, never control characters, never the excluded `a`.                   |
| `anchors_are_stripped_at_the_edges_and_empty_elsewhere`  | `^abc$` gives `abc`; a mid-pattern `\b` is empty.                                                        |
| `empty_ascii_intersection_uses_the_original_class`       | `\p{Han}{3}` gives three non-ASCII characters.                                                           |
| `open_repeat_is_capped_by_the_length_budget`             | `a+` under a budget of 3 stays within it, `[a-z]*` under 0 is empty, `a*` adds at most 8.                |
| `an_open_repeat_reaches_the_minimum_length`              | `^[A-Za-z0-9]+$` aimed at 10 to 12 characters gives 10 to 12.                                            |
| `hostile_nested_repeat_is_unsupported`                   | `(a{1000}){1000}` and `a{100000}` are refused.                                                           |
| `lookaround_and_backreference_are_unsupported`           | `(?=a)b`, `(a)\1` and `[` are refused.                                                                   |
| `is_match_agrees_with_the_validator_not_the_regex_crate` | `Müller` does not match `^\w+$` (ASCII `\w`).                                                            |
| `compiled_patterns_are_cached`                           | The same pattern text gives the same `Arc`; an unsupported one is cached as an error.                    |
| `same_seed_same_output_and_golden`                       | Five patterns x three seeds repeat and equal `tests/snapshots/pattern.json`.                             |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/generate/reconcile.rs` — time chains (D2)

The reconcile pass ([source](files/kaanal-data/generate.md#reconcile-d2)).

| Test                                                                 | What it proves                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `updated_not_before_created_over_1000_seeds`                         | created <= updated <= deleted < 2026-01-01 for 1000 seeds.                                                    |
| `every_builtin_chain_is_ordered`                                     | start/end, ordered/paid, paid/shipped, shipped/delivered and issued/due are ordered.                          |
| `adding_updated_at_does_not_change_created_at`                       | The earlier member is identical with and without the later one.                                               |
| `adding_an_unrelated_property_changes_nothing_else`                  | An extra `name` property leaves both timestamps unchanged.                                                    |
| `date_only_chain_is_ordered_and_stays_a_date`                        | `startDate`/`endDate` stay 10-character dates in order.                                                       |
| `a_date_and_a_date_time_are_left_alone`                              | A mixed-shape chain is skipped; the date equals its value without the other member.                           |
| `a_pinned_earlier_member_is_kept_and_later_ones_derive_from_it`      | An `example` creation time stays; `updatedAt` is after it and before the base date.                           |
| `a_pinned_later_member_is_never_changed`                             | An `enum` on `updatedAt` is never rewritten.                                                                  |
| `project_static_entry_pins_a_member`                                 | A `static` entry in `data.yaml` pins `createdAt`.                                                             |
| `project_chains_append_and_chains_off_removes`                       | A project chain orders its names; `chains_off` removes the built-in chain.                                    |
| `chains_need_two_names_and_non_empty_keys`                           | One name is a load warning; an empty name is an error.                                                        |
| `the_empty_form_keeps_the_ordered_scalars_of_the_full_body`          | `empty` equals the full body for a timestamps-only object.                                                    |
| `a_project_suffix_static_entry_pins_members`                         | A `"*at"` `static` entry (a suffix key) pins `createdAt` and `updatedAt`: neither is rewritten.               |
| `a_project_generator_that_is_not_static_or_pick_is_not_pinned`       | A project `timestamp` entry for `created_at` still lets the chain order the fields.                           |
| `a_project_chain_overlapping_a_builtin_one_keeps_the_builtin_order`  | `chains: [[created_at, deleted_at]]` next to the built-in chain leaves `createdAt <= updatedAt <= deletedAt`. |
| `an_impossible_pinned_date_is_not_chained`                           | A pinned `2025-13-45T99:00:00Z` is not parsed, so the chain leaves the other member alone.                    |
| `a_member_whose_pattern_the_derived_value_would_break_is_left_alone` | `updatedAt` with a `pattern` keeps a value that matches it.                                                   |
| `reconcile_is_idempotent` (unit, `reconcile/mod.rs`)                 | Running the pass on its own output changes nothing, 500 seeds.                                                |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/generate/reconcile_money.rs` — money sums (D2)

Line amounts, subtotal, tax, shipping, discount and total ([source](files/kaanal-data/generate.md#reconcile-d2)).

| Test                                                                                                                                                                          | What it proves                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `total_equals_sum_of_lines_exactly`                                                                                                                                           | 500 seeds: every line is price x quantity in cents, `subtotal` is their sum, `total` is subtotal + tax + shipping - capped discount, no value has more than two decimals. |
| `tax_is_a_fraction_of_the_subtotal`                                                                                                                                           | A free `tax` is at most about 20 percent of the subtotal.                                                                                                                 |
| `integer_money_uses_integer_math`                                                                                                                                             | All-integer money: the subtotal is the exact integer sum and `total` stays an integer.                                                                                    |
| `mixed_integer_and_number_skips`                                                                                                                                              | An integer price with a number subtotal leaves the subtotal as generated.                                                                                                 |
| `plain_listing_total_untouched`                                                                                                                                               | `items: [{name, price}]` with an integer `total` keeps the count.                                                                                                         |
| `bare_total_without_line_items_untouched`                                                                                                                                     | `subtotal` + `total` without lines: nothing derived.                                                                                                                      |
| `paged_envelope_total_stays_the_item_count`                                                                                                                                   | A page of line-item records reports `total` = the window total.                                                                                                           |
| `discount_is_capped_at_the_subtotal_and_the_total_never_negative`                                                                                                             | A free discount never exceeds the subtotal; the cap applies at least once in 300 seeds.                                                                                   |
| `a_pinned_discount_is_kept_and_total_still_never_negative`                                                                                                                    | An `example` discount stays; the total is not negative.                                                                                                                   |
| `overflow_skips_the_object`                                                                                                                                                   | `i64::MAX` x `i64::MAX` leaves the object as generated.                                                                                                                   |
| `bounds_win`                                                                                                                                                                  | A subtotal bound the sum would break keeps the generated value inside it.                                                                                                 |
| `a_pinned_line_amount_is_counted_not_rewritten`                                                                                                                               | An `example` line amount stays and is what the subtotal adds.                                                                                                             |
| `adding_an_unrelated_property_changes_no_money_field`                                                                                                                         | An extra `note` leaves every other field identical.                                                                                                                       |
| `an_integer_total_beside_line_items_alone_is_a_count`                                                                                                                         | Line items plus an integer `total` and no other money field: `total` is the generated count.                                                                              |
| `a_float_total_beside_line_items_alone_is_summed`                                                                                                                             | The same with a `number` total is the sum of the lines.                                                                                                                   |
| `a_non_numeric_pinned_field_skips_the_object_before_anything_is_written`                                                                                                      | A pinned `tax: "n/a"` leaves the subtotal and every line amount as generated.                                                                                             |
| `a_total_that_must_be_a_multiple_is_left_alone_when_the_sum_is_not_one`                                                                                                       | A `total` with `multipleOf: 7919` stays a multiple.                                                                                                                       |
| unit, `reconcile/mod.rs`: `a_budget_null_element_skips_the_sum`, `several_arrays_use_the_preferred_name_or_skip`, `an_empty_array_is_not_summed`, `money_sums_are_idempotent` | The skip rules and idempotence on hand-built objects.                                                                                                                     |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/generate/reconcile_person.rs` — age, birth date, locale facts (D2)

| Test                                                                             | What it proves                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `age_matches_dob_over_1000_seeds`                                                | Whole years from the date to 2026-01-01 equal `age`.                                                  |
| `age_stays_the_value_it_would_have_alone`                                        | The date follows the age; `age` is the value it has without a date.                                   |

| `a_pinned_date_derives_the_age`                                                  | `1990-05-17` gives 35.                                                                                |
| `the_age_rule_is_skipped_when_its_bounds_would_break`                            | A 2020 date with `minimum: 18` keeps the age inside 18..80.                                           |
| `both_pinned_are_left_alone`                                                     | Two examples stay as written.                                                                         |

| `a_date_time_on_the_edge_day_is_exact_to_the_second`                             | A date-time born on the base date's birthday is at midnight, so the age is exact.                     |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/generate/multiple.rs` and `unique.rs` — `multipleOf`, `uniqueItems` (D2)

Numbers on a grid and arrays without duplicates ([source](files/kaanal-data/generate.md#multipleof-and-uniqueitems-d2)).

| Test                                                                        | What it proves                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `money_multiple_of_a_cent_is_exact`                                         | `multipleOf: 0.01` over 1000 seeds gives whole cents inside 0..1000, no float modulo.                 |
| `a_quarter_step_gives_only_quarters`                                        | `0.25` gives multiples of a quarter within the bounds.                                                |
| `integer_multiple_of_five_within_bounds`                                    | `5` between 7 and 93 gives multiples of 5 inside the bounds.                                          |
| `exclusive_bounds_are_respected`                                            | `exclusiveMinimum`/`exclusiveMaximum` (3.0 booleans) keep the multiple strictly inside.               |
| `a_pinned_value_is_not_moved`                                               | `example` and `enum` values are kept even when not a multiple.                                        |
| `no_multiple_that_fits_warns_and_skips`                                     | `multipleOf: 10` between 1 and 9: a startup warning, the value stays in bounds.                       |
| `a_fractional_step_on_an_integer_field_warns_unless_every_integer_meets_it` | `multipleOf: 0.5` on an integer is a warning.                                                         |
| `bounds_with_float_error_still_admit_their_multiple`                        | `minimum: 1.1` (110.00000000000001 cents) and `0.57` (56.99999999999999) still allow their own value. |
| `bounds_beyond_the_counting_range_do_not_panic`                             | `minimum: 1e300` with `multipleOf: 2` warns and generates without overflow.                           |
| `a_usable_multiple_of_has_no_warning`                                       | A fitting `0.01` has no warning.                                                                      |
| `adding_a_sibling_property_does_not_change_the_snapped_value`               | R4.4 for a snapped number.                                                                            |
| `unique_scalar_enum_array_has_no_duplicates`                                | A unique enum array of 3..5 has no duplicates over 500 seeds and keeps its length.                    |
| `unique_strings_and_integers_have_no_duplicates`                            | Six unique strings or integers (1..8) never repeat.                                                   |
| `too_few_distinct_values_emit_what_is_possible_and_warn`                    | Two enum values with `minItems: 5`: warned, and the array has at most 2 unique items.                 |
| `a_redraw_under_a_one_of_never_becomes_null`                                | Duplicates among `oneOf` scalar items are redrawn to a real value, never `null`.                      |
| `unique_items_without_an_items_schema_still_dedupes`                        | No `items` means plain strings, deduped and not warned.                                               |
| `unique_items_on_objects_still_warns`                                       | An array of objects keeps a (new) specific warning.                                                   |
| `unique_scalars_have_no_warning_when_enough_values_exist`                   | Three enum values with `minItems: 2`: no warning.                                                     |
| `an_array_without_unique_items_is_unchanged`                                | `uniqueItems: false` equals the plain array, seed for seed.                                           |
| `adding_a_property_elsewhere_does_not_change_the_unique_array`              | R4.4 for a unique array.                                                                              |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/generate/dist.rs` — distributions and weighted choices (D2)

([source](files/kaanal-data/generate.md#distrs-d2)).

| Test                                                 | What it proves                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `weights_are_validated_when_the_dictionary_loads`    | All-zero weights, negative weights, the list form and `days: 0` are errors; unquoted `true`/`false` keys load. |
| `enum_weights_keep_every_value_reachable`            | A `status` enum over 10 000 seeds: paid > pending > cancelled, and the unlisted value still appears.           |
| `weights_on_pick_and_enum_share_one_shape`           | One `weights` map skews a project `pick` and a spec `enum` alike (about 90 percent `a`).                       |
| `a_project_plain_entry_turns_weights_off`            | A project `status` entry without weights makes the enum uniform.                                               |
| `booleans_are_mostly_true_for_active_flags`          | `active` is true about 80 percent of the time.                                                                 |
| `ratings_lean_positive_and_every_star_is_possible`   | Over 60 percent are 4 or 5 and all five values occur.                                                          |
| `quantity_is_mostly_one_to_three`                    | Over 65 percent are at most 3 and a value of 6 or more still occurs.                                           |
| `money_is_skewed_cheap_and_stays_in_bounds`          | 1 to 5000: over 60 percent below 1000 (uniform: 20), always inside the bounds, some above 2500.                |
| `a_range_from_zero_is_drawn_uniformly`               | `tax` 0 to 500: about 20 percent below 100.                                                                    |
| `retail_prices_end_in_99_and_00`                     | About half end in .99 and a quarter in .00.                                                                    |
| `retail_never_breaks_a_bound`                        | A price in 10.2 to 10.5 never leaves it.                                                                       |
| `retail_is_ignored_on_an_integer_field`              | Integer prices stay integers.                                                                                  |
| `a_project_entry_can_make_money_uniform_and_plain`   | `dist: uniform, style: plain` gives a flat spread.                                                             |
| `timestamps_lean_recent_inside_the_window`           | About two thirds fall in the last third of 365 days, none at or after the base date, the whole window is used. |
| `a_days_key_narrows_the_window`                      | `days: 7` keeps every value within a week.                                                                     |
| `days_and_years_have_sane_limits`                    | `days` over 36500 and unordered `years` are load errors.                                                       |
| `birth_dates_without_an_age_are_adults`              | A `format: date` `dateOfBirth` alone is 18 to 80 years before 2026-01-01.                                      |
| `whole_float_enum_values_match_whole_number_weights` | An enum of `1.0`/`2.0` is weighted by a `{ 1: 9, 2: 1 }` map.                                                  |
| `a_huge_money_range_and_a_tiny_minimum_still_draw`   | `0.01..1e12` can reach above 1e10; a subnormal minimum falls back to uniform instead of hanging.               |
| `ages_follow_the_four_weighted_bands`                | 18 to 80 only; the bands hold about 25/30/25/20 percent.                                                       |
| `same_seed_same_output_for_every_distribution`       | Repeatable for price, enum, timestamp and age.                                                                 |

Re-record with `KAANAL_RECORD=1`. |

| `configured_data_path_is_used_and_a_missing_one_is_an_error_at_data`       | `data:` is read relative to the config; a missing file is an error at `data:` with no file.                                                                                    |

| `schemas_accept_the_built_ins_and_reject_a_wrong_entry`                    | The dictionary and pack JSON Schemas accept the built-in files and a generator list, and reject an unknown generator or parameter, an empty list and a pack without `streets`. |
| `symlinks_in_locales_are_skipped_with_a_warning`                           | A symlinked pack in `locales/` is not loaded and warns "symlinks are not followed" (skipped where symlinks cannot be made).                                                    |
| `more_than_64_pack_files_is_an_error_at_the_directory`                     | 65 files in `locales/` fail at the directory with "65 pack files; at most 64".                                                                                                 |
| `yml_and_upper_case_yaml_files_are_skipped_with_a_warning`                 | `.yml` and `.YAML` packs are skipped with a warning each; other files are ignored silently.                                                                                    |
| `tags_that_differ_only_in_case_are_rejected_and_lookups_hint`              | A project `en-us` pack fails at its `tag:` value naming `en-US`; looking up `en-us` hints "did you mean `en-US`?".                                                             |
| `a_configured_data_path_that_is_not_a_file_is_an_error_at_data`            | `data:` naming a directory fails at `data:`.                                                                                                                                   |

## `crates/kaanal-data/src/warn.rs` (unit) + `tests/warnings.rs` — ignored-keyword warnings

| Test                                                   | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| `flags_ignored_keywords_but_not_property_names`        | A _property called_ `pattern` is not flagged; a real `not:` keyword is, with locations.                                                                                                               |
| `walks_items_and_combinators`                          | `not` nested inside `items → allOf` is found.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `flags_max_length_that_cannot_fit_the_format`          | `format: uuid` + `maxLength: 10` warns (the format wins); `maxLength: 36` does not.                                                                                                                   |
| `flags_min_length_over_the_cap_or_the_format`          | (unit) `minLength: 20000` warns "capped at 10000"; `uuid` + `minLength: 50` warns (the format wins); `minLength: 10000` and `email` + `minLength` do not.                                             |
| `allof_type_conflict_warns_once_per_endpoint`          | (unit) Two properties with `allOf: [{type: string}, {type: integer}]` give exactly one "allOf types conflict; first wins" warning; matching types give none.                                          |
| `clean_schema_has_no_warnings`                         | No ignored keywords → no warnings.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `too_deep_schema_warns_once_and_matches_the_generator` | (unit) 16 levels: no warning and a filled leaf; 17 levels on two branches: one warning per endpoint, and the generator returns `null` at that depth.                                                  |
| `api_model_collects_warnings_per_endpoint`             | End-to-end from a spec string: `ApiModel::warnings()` prefixes warnings with `GET /things`.                                                                                                           |
| `max_length_below_a_new_format_warns`                  | (unit) `maxLength` too small for `ipv4` (5), `byte` (8) or `duration` (4) warns; `ipv4` at 11 does not. The discriminator warnings are tested in `tests/generate/` (`one_of.rs`) (`discriminator_*`). |
| `two_array_properties_warn_once`                       | A paged object with several arrays gives one warning naming the paged one; one array, or an unpaged route, gives none.                                                                                |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/empty.rs` — empty bodies

The `empty` / `empty_of_value` rules behind the `empty` pin state ([source](files/kaanal-data/empty.md)).

| Test                                                           | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `array_schema_is_empty_list`                                   | An `array` schema gives `[]`.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `envelope_with_data_and_meta`                                  | `{data: array, meta: {total: integer}}` gives `{data: [], meta: {total: 0}}`: arrays emptied, nested objects recursed, `total` reset.                                                               |
| `pagination_names_are_case_insensitive`                        | `TotalCount` (integer) gives 0, `has_more` (boolean) false, `NextCursor` (string) null.                                                                                                             |
| `other_scalars_are_generated_and_stable_per_seed`              | `page`, `limit` and `name` have no rule: they are generated (integers and a string), identical for the same seed.                                                                                   |
| `oneof_and_bare_scalar_have_no_empty_form`                     | A top-level `oneOf`, `anyOf`, `allOf` or a bare `string` gives `None`.                                                                                                                              |
| `object_without_properties_is_empty_object`                    | `{type: object}` gives `{}`.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `value_form_array_object_scalar`                               | `empty_of_value`: `[1,2]` gives `[]`; `{items:[1], total:5, name:"x"}` gives `{items:[], total:0, name:"x"}`; a bare number gives `None`.                                                           |
| `name_rules_respect_property_type`                             | A string called `size` or `has_more` is generated, not zeroed; `next` is `null` whatever its type; in the value form `{size:"L", has_more:"yes", count:3}` keeps the strings and zeroes the number. |
| `other_table_names_and_nested_value_arrays`                    | `totalItems`, `numFound` (a `number`), `hasNext` and `nextPage` follow the table; in the value form a nested `page` object has its rows emptied and `hasNext` set false.                            |
| `union_types_act_like_their_non_null_member`                   | `type: [integer, null]`, `[boolean, null]`, `[array, null]` and `[object, null]` properties take the rule of the non-null member, nested; an untyped `size: {}` is not zeroed.                      |
| `top_level_union_types`                                        | `[array, null]` at the top gives `[]`, `[object, null]` with properties gives `{total: 0}`, `[string, null]` gives `None`.                                                                          |
| `the_options_pack_and_project_dictionary_reach_the_empty_form` | With `en-US` and a project `data.yaml` entry, the empty form has `USD` and the entry's value; the default options give `INR`.                                                                       |
| `empty_form_scalars_match_generate_and_keep_sibling_seeds`     | Kept scalars (top level and nested) equal `generate`'s for 40 seeds, two identical `oneOf` properties do not always pick together, and adding a property changes no other value (R4.4).             |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/state.rs` — load pipeline (no HTTP)

| Test                                                          | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `load_errors_carry_their_yaml_place`                          | A bad spec path is placed at `nodes.app.spec`; a static route with no gateway is placed at its `endpoints:` key.                                                                                                          |
| `fixture_config_is_valid`                                     | The shared fixture passes `check`.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `missing_spec_file_is_reported_with_path`                     | A missing spec file errors, naming the file path.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `same_route_in_two_services_conflicts`                        | Two services defining `GET /users/{id}` → error naming both.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `malformed_route_key_is_rejected`                             | An `endpoints:` key without `"METHOD /path"` shape fails.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `topology_errors_surface`                                     | Two gateways surface through `check` with "at most one gateway".                                                                                                                                                          |
| `spec_on_non_service_is_rejected`                             | `spec` on a gateway is an unknown-field parse error.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `unsupported_keywords_become_warnings`                        | The `not` keyword in `openapi-warn.yaml` becomes exactly one warning naming the file.                                                                                                                                     |
| `clean_config_has_no_warnings`                                | The clean fixture produces zero warnings.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `concurrency_no_longer_warns_in_live_mode`                    | `concurrency` on a gateway and a service produces no warning mentioning it: live mode enforces it through the engine actor.                                                                                               |
| `token_is_64_hex_and_random`                                  | `new_token`: 64 hex chars, two calls differ.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `override_differing_only_in_param_name_is_a_clear_error`      | `GET /users/{x}` vs spec's `GET /users/{id}` → error naming both spellings.                                                                                                                                               |
| `override_with_same_param_name_replaces_the_spec_route`       | Same shape, same names → override merges cleanly.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `override_needs_known_method_and_leading_slash`               | `FOO /x` and `GET users` each fail with targeted messages.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `override_status_must_be_a_real_http_status`                  | Status 99, 100, 199 and 1000 are rejected (200–599 only).                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `control_namespace_is_reserved`                               | `endpoints:` can never claim `/__kaanal` or anything under it.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `gatewayless_topology_validates`                              | Service → database with no gateway passes `check`.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `static_route_without_gateway_is_rejected`                    | Without a gateway an `endpoints:` key no service owns fails "static route needs a gateway or a service that owns it"; overriding an owned route is fine.                                                                  |
| `head_override_keeps_the_owning_service`                      | R6.8: a `HEAD` override of an owned path loads with or without a gateway (it keeps GET's owner); a `PATCH` on an owned path with no gateway is still a static route and is rejected.                                      |
| `endpoints_file_errors_name_the_file_and_place`               | A missing endpoints file is placed at the `endpoints:` value; a compile error inside the file carries that file and its position (BOM and CRLF change nothing); a shape error keeps serde's position (`Located::parsed`). |
| `stale_entries_and_ignored_error_maps_are_warnings`           | A stale entry and a `via:` target's ignored `error_map` come back from `check` as warnings naming the endpoint.                                                                                                           |
| `spec_node_without_a_gateway_edge_is_rejected_before_compile` | P4: a spec node with no edge from the gateway fails in the topology check, naming the node, so `compile` never sees a spec route with no place.                                                                           |
| `auth_without_users_warns`                                    | An auth node with no `users` makes `check` return the "no users, so every POST /login answers 401" warning; one user silences it.                                                                                         |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/http/` — end-to-end HTTP

One test target (`cargo test -p kaanal-server --test http`), one module per topic in `tests/http/` (R3.8):

| Module         | What it covers                                                                                             |
| -------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `common.rs`    | Shared helpers: starting a server with a config, a seed or a signing key.                                  |
| `mock.rs`      | Mock routes: generated and static answers, 404 and 405, CORS, HEAD, path parameters.                       |
| `chaos_api.rs` | The chaos control routes: set, patch, clear, validate, per-endpoint keys.                                  |
| `guard.rs`     | The control guard: token, Host, query token, caching headers.                                              |
| `events_ui.rs` | Topology, the event stream and the UI page.                                                                |
| `chains.rs`    | Traces and chains: seeds, gatewayless and monolith configs, error maps, endpoints files, realistic replay. |
| `auth.rs`      | The auth node: login, JWKS, error cases, CORS and secrecy.                                                 |
| `capacity.rs`  | Pools, concurrency and rate limits.                                                                        |
| `wire.rs`      | Timeouts and wire failures (reset, malformed).                                                             |

Each test boots a real server on an OS-chosen port (`Srv` helper) against the fixtures. Every test server gets a clone of one signing key shared by the test binary. The login tests use the `AUTH` fixture (gateway plus an auth node with `proc` 50 ms and two users) and a `verify` helper that checks a token against the served JWKS with `jsonwebtoken`, an independent implementation.

_Mocks:_

| Test                                                                        | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `users_42_is_stable_traced_and_uses_path_id`                                | `GET /users/42` twice returns identical bodies; `id` is 42 (param applied); trace header starts `nginx.proc=` and contains `db.read=`; email ends `@example.com`.                                                         |
| `non_numeric_id_for_integer_field_still_returns_200`                        | `/users/abc` keeps a generated numeric id.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `static_override_beats_generated`                                           | The fixture's `"GET /users": [ { id: 1 } ]` override wins over the spec schema.                                                                                                                                           |
| `static_only_route_works`                                                   | `/health` exists only via override and returns 200 `{ ok: true }`.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `post_orders_returns_201`                                                   | Status comes from the spec (201); body is generated (currency INR).                                                                                                                                                       |
| `unknown_and_trailing_slash_paths_are_404`                                  | `/nope`, `/users/42/`, `/__kaanal/nope` all 404 `no route`.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `wrong_method_is_405`                                                       | DELETE on a GET-only path → 405.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `mocks_send_cors_headers_and_answer_preflight`                              | `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` on responses and 204 preflights.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `head_answers_like_get_without_a_body`                                      | HEAD answers like GET (200 + trace header) with an empty body.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `head_has_its_own_counter_and_never_shifts_get`                             | R6.8: with 50 % db errors, 8 GETs with a HEAD after each give the same status sequence as 8 GETs alone (both 200 and 503 occur, so the check is not vacuous).                                                             |
| `percent_encoded_path_param_is_decoded_before_use`                          | `/users/%34%32` behaves as `/users/42`.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `trace_shows_sub_millisecond_hops_and_names_the_failing_node`               | Every hop shows one decimal (`1.0ms`, never `0ms`); with db chaos at 100 %, the trace ends `db.error=504`.                                                                                                                |
| `timeout_returns_504_near_the_deadline_with_kind_in_the_body`               | A gateway `timeout: 200ms` over a slow database answers 504 near 200 ms with `"kind":"timeout"` in the default body and a trace ending `error=504`.                                                                       |
| `status_failure_body_is_unchanged`                                          | A plain status failure keeps today's body: no `kind` field.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `hang_with_a_short_node_timeout_answers_504`                                | Chaos `hang` on a node with a short `timeout:` answers 504 at that timeout, not after 60 s.                                                                                                                               |
| `deep_reset_and_malformed_become_502`                                       | `reset` or `malformed` at the database (a deep node) is a 502.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `reset_on_the_owner_behind_a_gateway_aborts_the_connection`                 | Raw socket: reset at the owner (a wire node) sends the head and part of the body, then EOF short of `Content-Length`.                                                                                                     |
| `malformed_on_a_wire_node_is_valid_http_with_invalid_json`                  | Malformed at a wire node: the route's success status, correct `Content-Length`, a body that does not parse as JSON.                                                                                                       |
| `malformed_on_head_answers_the_plain_response`                              | HEAD (and 204/304) have nothing to cut: the plain success response, same declared length as the GET.                                                                                                                      |
| `error_map_kind_key_overrides_the_wire_behaviour`                           | An `error_map` key `reset` (or `malformed`) turns a wire failure into the mapped JSON response.                                                                                                                           |
| `class_key_does_not_swallow_a_wire_reset`                                   | A `5xx` key does not catch a wire reset: only node and kind keys apply there (Z8).                                                                                                                                        |
| `endpoint_and_node_at_chaos_through_the_control_api`                        | `PUT /chaos` with `endpoint` and with `node`+`endpoint`: effect only on that route; `DELETE ?node=&endpoint=` removes exactly that key and leaves node chaos; unknown endpoint 404; bad `error_kind` 400; bad status 400. |
| `get_chaos_is_nested`                                                       | `GET /__kaanal/chaos` returns `{nodes, endpoints, node_at}`.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `trace_names_reset_and_malformed_and_no_body_routes_get_the_plain_response` | The trace ends `node.error=reset` / `malformed`; a body-less 204 route answers plain.                                                                                                                                     |
| `error_map_keyed_by_node_name_overrides_a_wire_reset`                       | An `error_map` entry under the failing node's name beats a wire reset.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `endpoint_level_reset_belongs_to_the_gateway_and_aborts`                    | Endpoint-wide reset: the failure is the gateway's and the connection aborts.                                                                                                                                              |
| `a_gateway_timeout_goes_through_error_map_like_any_failure`                 | A timeout at a wire node is not a wire failure: `error_map` (`504`, `5xx`, node keys) remaps it; the default is a plain 504.                                                                                              |
| `malformed_on_an_auth_responder_cuts_the_real_reply`                        | Malformed on the JWKS route cuts the real JWKS reply.                                                                                                                                                                     |

_Control API & security:_

| Test                                                           | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `chaos_on_db_fails_requests_then_clears`                       | PUT 100 % errors → mocks return 503 `{"error":"chaos","node":"db"}`; DELETE → mocks recover.                                                                                                                 |
| `chaos_patch_merges_and_lists`                                 | Two PUTs merge (`latency_ms` + `down` both kept); GET lists the merged view; DELETE all empties it.                                                                                                          |
| `latency_chaos_slows_requests`                                 | +200 ms chaos makes a real request take ≥ 200 ms wall time.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `control_requires_token`                                       | No token and wrong token both → 401.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `control_rejects_foreign_host`                                 | `Host: evil.example` → 403.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `host_guard_covers_the_whole_control_namespace`                | Even the token-free `health` and `ui` reject a foreign Host.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `unauthenticated_calls_are_rejected_before_the_body_is_parsed` | A PUT with malformed JSON and no token gets 401 (not 400) — the guard runs first.                                                                                                                            |
| `query_token_only_works_on_events`                             | `?token=` on `/topology` → 401.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `control_responses_are_not_cacheable`                          | `Cache-Control: no-store` + `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`.                                                                                                                                               |
| `unknown_node_is_404_with_message`                             | Chaos on `redis` → 404 `unknown node 'redis'`.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `bad_chaos_values_are_4xx_with_json_errors`                    | rate 1.5, status 418, latency 300 001 ms, negative latency, unknown keys — all 4xx with a JSON `error` field; range errors are exactly 400; a rejected patch stores nothing; exactly 300 000 ms is accepted. |
| `topology_lists_nodes_edges_routes`                            | `/topology` returns the fixture's nodes, edges and the 4 sorted route keys.                                                                                                                                  |
