| `reset_clears_live_pins`                                                   | `POST /reset` empties the live pins and the route answers normally again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `live_cycle_does_not_reach_a_scoped_request`                               | With a live `error` pin the plain request is 404 while scoped requests see only their scenarios' pins (503, 200) or none (`db-down` gives its chain 502); a later live change is seen at once by plain requests despite the scoped cache.                                                                                |
| `concurrent_cycles_on_two_endpoints_both_land`                             | Twenty rounds of two simultaneous cycles on different endpoints: both are 200 and both live pins exist.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `save_writes_pins_and_reload_round_trips`                                  | Saving from `live`, `effective` and `{scenario}` writes `pins:` (and no `chaos:` for live-only pins; a delay as `1s 500ms`); nothing to save is 400; `clear_live` clears the live pins; a fresh server over the same directory reads the files and the pins behave (empty body or 503) as saved.                         |
| `scenario_events_for_pin_and_cycle`                                        | The scenario SSE stream carries `pin` events (PUT and DELETE) and a `cycle` event, each with the endpoint as `name`.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `concurrent_cycles_on_one_endpoint_walk_distinct_consecutive_states`       | Fifty rounds of five barrier-released cycles on one endpoint return the five states exactly once each (the step is computed under the control lock).                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `no_op_deletes_publish_no_event_and_real_ones_do`                          | Deleting an absent, unknown or empty live layer is 204 and sends no event; only the real removal and the real clear-all do (four `pin` events with the two puts).                                                                                                                                                        |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/logs_http.rs` — ids, events, logs and trace

Request ids, the event bus and the two log routes ([log](files/kaanal-server/log.md), [log_api](files/kaanal-server/log_api.md), [mock](files/kaanal-server/mock.md)). Own harness (`Srv`, `Events`): a gateway, a service with a spec, a database, a rate-limited service and two external APIs, with `GET /users` a `parallel` block, `POST /orders` behind a 1 rps limit and `GET /users/{id}` ending at the database. `Events` subscribes to the SSE stream first and reads `request` events.

_Ids and events:_

| Test                                                  | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `every_mock_response_has_a_request_id`                | A `200`, the parallel route, a chaos `503`, a pinned `500`, a wire `reset`, a `404`, a `405`, a bad-scope `400` and a `429` each answer an `X-Request-Id` that matches `^r[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9]{4,}$`; the numbers are distinct; `Access-Control-Expose-Headers` lists `x-request-id`. |
| `control_responses_and_preflights_have_no_request_id` | `/__kaanal/health`, a control `404` and an `OPTIONS` preflight carry no `X-Request-Id`; the preflight took no number (the first real request is id 1).                                                                                                                             |
| `ids_increase_and_survive_reset`                      | Three requests with a `reset` between the second and third get numbers 1, 2, 3 with one boot nonce: reset does not restart the counter.                                                                                                                                            |
| `each_request_makes_exactly_one_event`                | With the stream open first, five requests (one a `404`), a bad-scope one and a last good one (a preflight and a control call in between) produce exactly seven `request` events, equal to the response ids, in order.                                                              |
| `client_request_id_is_recorded_and_a_bad_one_ignored` | `web-7f.a_b-1` and a 64-byte id are recorded as `client_id`; a 65-byte id, an id with a space and one whose bytes are not UTF-8 are not (`null`), and the response still carries Kaanal's own id.                                                                                  |
| `sse_event_carries_id_and_seq`                        | The event of a `200` has our `id`, `seq` equal to the id's number, `client_id` null and `rejected` null; the event of a `404` has `rejected: "unmatched"` and status 404.                                                                                                          |
| `bad_scope_is_observed_but_still_not_counted`         | A 50% error rate gives the same 12 statuses whether or not a bad-scope request precedes each one (no counter value, no draw), while the stream shows 12 `bad-scope` events among 36. (A bad scope used to leave no event; it now leaves one.)                                      |

_The log routes:_

| Test                                                            | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `logs_default_limit_and_order`                                  | 150 requests: with no `limit` the page is the last 100, `done` 51 to 150 oldest first; `oldest` ends `0001`, `newest` ends `0150`, `cap` is 1000; `limit=5` gives 5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `logs_filters`                                                  | Four requests (a `200`, a `404`, a chaos `503`, a `200` with an empty `X-Kaanal-Scenario`): `endpoint=GET /users/{id}` finds 3 and so does lower-case `get`; `status=404` 1, `5xx` 1, `404,5xx` 2; `failed=true` 1; `scope=` (empty) exactly 1 and `scope=nope` 0; `since=1h` 4, a future time 0, a past time 4; `after=<done>` the later ones; `since=40ms` after a pause excludes the older. |
| `logs_bad_input_is_400_and_names_the_parameter`                 | Thirteen bad queries (`bogus`, `limit` of 0, 1001, `abc`, empty, `+5`, `5`, huge and repeated, `status=abc`, `since=yesterday`, `failed=maybe`, empty `endpoint`) each answer `400` whose message contains the parameter; `limit=1000` on an empty ring gives `[]`.                                                                                                                            |
| `ring_stays_at_1000_and_reports_evicted`                        | 1200 requests (32 in flight at a time): `limit=1000` returns 1000, `evicted` is 200, and the very first request's id answers `404 { error: "evicted", detail: ... }`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `trace_by_id_last_and_client_id`                                | `/trace/{id}`, `/trace/last` and `/trace/web-7` (the client id, also percent-escaped as `web%2D7`) return the right entries, with `hops` and `trace`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `trace_evicted_vs_unknown_vs_malformed`                         | Another number of this boot, an id of another boot and an unsent client id are `404 { error: "unknown" }`; a space and bytes that are not UTF-8 are `400`; `last` after `reset` is `404`.                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `trace_of_an_out_of_order_slow_request_found_after_it_finishes` | A request held 400 ms by chaos while fast ones overtake it: `last` is a fast one while the slow one is in flight; afterwards the slow id (lower number) is found, and `last` is now the slow one (the last to finish).                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `routes_need_the_token`                                         | `/logs` and `/trace/last` without a token are `401`, and with a token but a foreign `Host` `403`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `logs_entries_have_no_bodies_or_query`                          | A request with `?token=secret-query-789`, an `Authorization` and a custom header carrying `secret-header-456`, and a body with `secret-body-123`: none of the three appears in `/logs` or `/trace`; the client id and the clean path do.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `reset_empties_logs_but_ids_continue`                           | After `reset` the page is `[]` with `oldest` and `newest` null; the next id is the previous number + 1; the id from before `reset` answers `evicted`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/shutdown_http.rs` — the drain over HTTP

Starts a real server on an OS-chosen port (the gateway, a service and a database, with `GET /users/{id}` served by `db`) and drives `POST /__kaanal/shutdown`. A `hang` fault on the database holds a request far longer than any test here; `ends_within` asserts that `BoundServer::wait` returns `Ok` in time. See [shutdown](files/kaanal-server/shutdown.md).

| Test                                                       | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `post_shutdown_answers_202_and_the_server_stops`           | No body: `202 {"shutting_down":true,"timeout_ms":5000}` and `wait` ends within 1 s; with `{"timeout":"2s"}` the answer says 2000.                                                                                      |
| `a_second_request_never_extends_the_deadline`              | With a hung request, a 400 ms shutdown, then (through the handle, since the listener is already closed) a 60 s request: the time left is at most 400 ms, the server ends within 2 s, and the held client got an error. |
| `bad_timeouts_are_400_naming_timeout`                      | `"abc"`, `"0s"`, `"301s"`, a number and a JSON array are 400 (an object's message names `timeout`); a body that is not JSON is 400; the server is still healthy afterwards.                                            |
| `shutdown_needs_the_token`                                 | No token: 401. A foreign `Host`: 403. `GET`: 405. A cross-origin `POST` gets no `access-control-allow-origin`. None of them stops the server.                                                                          |
| `in_flight_request_finishes_inside_the_budget`             | An 800 ms request started before the shutdown completes with 200 and the server then ends within 3 s.                                                                                                                  |
| `in_flight_request_is_cut_at_the_budget`                   | A hung request and a 200 ms budget: the shutdown call itself gets its `202`, `wait` ends no sooner than 100 ms and within 2 s, and the held client ends with an error instead of hanging.                              |
| `new_connections_are_refused_while_draining`               | While a hung request holds a 5 s drain open, a new TCP connection to the port fails within 2 s.                                                                                                                        |
| `events_stream_ends_when_shutdown_is_requested`            | An open `/events` stream receives `event: shutdown` with a `timeout_ms` between 1 ms and 60 s and then ends; the server ends within 3 s despite the 60 s budget (the stream does not hold the drain).                  |
| `other_connections_still_drain_with_an_events_stream_open` | With an events stream open and an 800 ms request in flight, the request still completes with 200, the stream still ends with `event: shutdown`, and the server ends within 3 s.                                        |
| `a_whitespace_body_is_no_body`                             | A body of a space, a newline and a tab answers `202` with `timeout_ms` 5000.                                                                                                                                           |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/studio_assets/` — Studio's page and assets

Uses a small fixture bundle (`tests/fixtures/studio-dist`, `studio-shapes`) and an injected table, so no build with `cfg(kaanal_ui)` is needed. `page.rs`: the page; `routes.rs`: the assets route.

| Test                                                     | What it proves                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `page_has_csp_and_no_referrer`                           | The page carries the CSP and `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`.                                                                                         |
| `build_without_bundle_serves_status_page`                | With no bundle `/__kaanal/studio` is the status page and assets are 404.                                                                             |
| `studio_serves_the_bundle_with_the_right_content_types`  | The page and each asset answer 200 with the right `Content-Type`.                                                                                    |
| `the_assets_need_no_token_but_the_api_still_does`        | A table hit needs no token; `/__kaanal/topology` still gets 401.                                                                                     |
| `a_sibling_of_an_exempt_path_still_needs_the_token`      | Every other path under `/__kaanal/studio/` is exactly 401 without a token and 404 with one; the page is 200 and `studio2` unchanged.                 |
| `the_exempt_paths_still_refuse_a_bad_host`               | A wrong `Host` is 403 even on the page and assets.                                                                                                   |
| `table_hit_needs_no_token_get_and_head_only`             | `GET` and `HEAD` pass without a token; `POST` and `OPTIONS` get 401.                                                                                 |
| `head_content_length_matches_get`                        | `HEAD` reports the `GET` length for identity and `br`.                                                                                               |
| `svg_carries_its_own_csp`                                | An SVG answer has the restrictive CSP.                                                                                                               |
| `miss_is_401_without_token_404_with_it`                  | A name not in the table: 401 without a token, 404 with one.                                                                                          |
| `hashed_asset_is_immutable_and_vary_set`                 | A hashed file is `immutable` and every variant has `Vary: Accept-Encoding`; an unhashed icon is `no-cache`.                                          |
| `if_none_match_gives_304`                                | A matching `If-None-Match` answers 304 with ETag, cache and Vary and no body.                                                                        |
| `index_html_and_map_and_manifest_not_served_from_assets` | `index.html`, `.map` and the manifest are never served from the assets route.                                                                        |
| `asset_path_variants_fall_through_to_auth`               | `..`, encoded dots and slashes, case, trailing and double slash and `?query` variants (raw socket, GET and HEAD) are all 401 or 404, never the file. |

Unit tests in `src/control/assets.rs` (`lookup_is_exact`, `hit_needs_get_or_head_no_query_and_the_raw_prefix`, `negotiate_*`, `etags_differ_per_encoding`, `if_none_match_compares_weakly_and_takes_lists`, `cache_control_is_keyed_by_file`) and `src/control/manifest.rs` (allowlist, exclusions, hashed rules including the real Vite sample, a dot segment and a missing file failing the build, generation, content types). `src/control/tests.rs` adds `guard_keeps_a_cache_header_the_handler_set`.

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/site.rs` — the `site:` folder

The `site:` key through `check`, then a real server (a gateway, a service with the users/orders spec, a database, `site: web`) with `secret.txt` next to `web/` and one level above. `Srv::raw` sends a request line byte for byte, since reqwest would resolve `..` and `\` before sending. See [site](files/kaanal-server/site.md).

| Test                                                     | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `site_must_be_a_relative_folder_inside_the_project`      | An absolute path, `..` out, `.`, `./`, an empty value, a file and a missing folder each fail at the `site:` line, naming the key and the value.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `a_site_symlink_to_outside_is_rejected` _(unix)_         | A `site:` folder that is a symlink out of the project fails "inside".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `site_loads_a_folder_and_check_passes`                   | `web` and `./web/` load.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `no_site_key_means_no_site`                              | No key, no error.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `index_html_at_root`                                     | `/` is `index.html` with `text/html; charset=utf-8`, `no-cache`, `nosniff`, no CORS header and no `X-Request-Id`; a folder with an `index.html` answers with it with or without the slash.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `file_by_path_with_content_type`                         | html, css, js, json, svg, png, ico and woff2 get their types; an unknown extension and no extension are `application/octet-stream`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `head_has_headers_and_no_body`                           | `HEAD` gets the type and the file's `Content-Length`, and no body.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `post_is_not_served_by_the_site`                         | `POST /index.html` is the mock's 404.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `a_spec_route_beats_a_file_with_the_same_path`           | A `web/users` file does not shadow `GET /users` (the mock answers, with an id); a `web/orders` file does not turn the spec's `POST`-only `/orders` into a `GET`: still 405.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `unknown_path_gets_the_mock_404`                         | A missing file is the mock's `{"error":"no route"}` 404.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `no_directory_listing`                                   | A folder without `index.html`, with or without the slash, is the mock's 404.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `chaos_on_every_node_does_not_touch_the_site`            | With `error_rate: 1` on every node `GET /users` is 503 and `/` is still 200 with the page.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `site_requests_are_not_logged`                           | Two `GET`s and a `HEAD` of site files leave `/__kaanal/logs` unchanged; a fall-through 404 adds one entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `nothing_under_the_control_prefix_is_served`             | `web/__kaanal/index.html` and `web/__kaanal/x.txt` are never sent, are a 404, and no CORS header appears; the encoded `/%5f%5fkaanal/x.txt`, `/__kaanal%2fx.txt` and the case variant `/__KAANAL/x.txt` are the mock's 404 without the secret.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `traversal_never_returns_file_bytes`                     | `..` raw, `%2e%2e`, `..%2f`, `%252e%252e` (decoded once), `%5c`, `c:`, `C:%2f`, `%00` (also after a real file), `:$DATA`, `::$DATA` (also `%3a%3a`), the `CON` and `NUL` device names (bare, with an extension, in a subfolder), `%2f%2f`, and the secret's absolute path (as `/abs`, `//abs` and `%2f`-encoded) are all the mock's 404 without the secret; a raw `\` is 400 or 404; the same helper reads a real file. |
| `names_that_only_resemble_the_control_prefix_are_served` | `__kaanal2.txt`, `_kaanal` and `a/__kaanal/x.txt` are served with 200, so the first-segment guard is not over-broad.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `a_symlink_out_of_the_site_is_not_followed` _(unix)_     | A file link and a folder link out of `web/` are the mock's 404; a link that stays inside is served.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/collections.rs` — collection routes

`Collections::apply` directly, then a real server (a gateway, a notes service and a database; `GET /notes`, `POST /notes`, `GET /notes/{id}` and `DELETE /notes/{id}` are all `collection: notes` with a `db` hop). See [collections](files/kaanal-server/collections.md).

| Test                                                 | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `add_list_get_remove_roundtrip`                      | Add is 201 with the item and its `id`; list keeps insertion order; get by id; ids `3`, `0`, `-1`, `x`, empty and a 26-digit number are 404; remove is 204 and a second remove 404; another name is a separate, empty collection.                                                                                       |
| `ids_are_sequential_and_ignore_a_client_id`          | A client `id` (number or string) is overwritten; a removed id is not handed out again; each collection counts from 1.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `bad_oversize_and_full_collection_change_nothing`    | An array, string, number, `null` or missing item is 400; over 64 KiB is 413; the 1001st add is 409; none of them takes an id. Over the wire: not-JSON, an array, cut-off JSON, 10 000-deep nesting and invalid UTF-8 are 400, a body over the cap 413, all with a trace header (the chain ran), and nothing is stored. |
| `fifty_concurrent_adds_get_distinct_ids`             | 50 parallel `POST`s get ids 1 to 50, each once, and the list has 50 items.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `reset_clears_every_collection`                      | `clear` empties two collections and restarts ids; over the wire `POST /__kaanal/reset` does the same.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `a_collection_get_still_goes_through_the_chain`      | With 300 ms of chaos latency on the owner, `GET /notes` takes at least that long, answers `[]` and its trace names `db`.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `a_chaos_failed_post_does_not_persist`               | A `POST` failed by a 503 on `db` stores nothing, and the next add gets id 1.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `a_wire_failed_post_does_not_persist_or_apply_twice` | A `malformed` failure on the owner answers 201 with a cut body (the preview), stores nothing and takes no id: the next real add is id 1 and the list has one item.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `a_pinned_error_post_does_not_persist`               | A `POST` under an `error` pin answers 500 and one under an `empty` pin 201 (so the pins really answered); neither stores anything.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `a_wire_failed_delete_does_not_report_success`       | Under `malformed` and `reset` on the owner, a `DELETE` (a 204, nothing to cut) and a `HEAD` by id answer 502 `{"error":"chaos","node":"app","kind":...}`, not a clean success; the item is still there afterwards.                                                                                                     |
| `a_failed_or_pinned_delete_removes_nothing`          | A `DELETE` failed by a 503 on `db` (503), pinned `error` (500) and pinned `empty` (204) removes nothing; an unpinned one then removes it.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ids_must_be_canonical`                              | `007`, `+7`, `7`, `7` and `07` are 404 for get and remove; `7` is found.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `a_head_of_a_missing_id_is_404_with_no_body`         | `HEAD /notes/1` on an empty collection is 404 with an empty body.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `a_pinned_loading_post_persists_once`                | Under a `loading` pin a `POST` answers 201 with id 1 and is stored exactly once.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `get_of_an_empty_collection_is_an_empty_array`       | `GET /notes` is `[]`, `HEAD` 200 with no body; get by id `1`, a huge id and `%FF` are 404 `{"error":"not found"}`; a `HEAD` by id reads and changes nothing.                                                                                                                                                           |

## `crates/kaanal-data/tests/page/` — paging (D3)

See [page](files/kaanal-data/page.md).

### `detect.rs` — style and bounds from an endpoint

| Test                                                | What it proves                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `page_param_gives_page_style`                       | `page` is the page style.                                                                                                    |
| `offset_and_skip_give_offset_style`                 | `offset` and `skip` are the offset style.                                                                                    |
| `cursor_after_pagetoken_give_cursor_style`          | `cursor`, `after`, `pageToken` are the cursor style.                                                                         |
| `names_are_normalised`                              | Case, `_` and `-` are ignored in names.                                                                                      |
| `offset_beats_page_when_both_declared`              | Offset wins over page.                                                                                                       |
| `cursor_beats_offset_and_page`                      | Cursor wins over offset and page.                                                                                            |
| `index_and_offset_aliases_are_normalised`           | `Page`, `page_number`, `PageNumber`, `Skip` are recognised.                                                                  |
| `size_aliases_are_recognised`                       | `size`, `count`, `maxResults` are size parameters.                                                                           |
| `no_recognised_param_is_none`                       | No paging parameter: not paged.                                                                                              |
| `size_defaults_and_bounds`                          | Default 20 or the spec's, max 100 or the spec's, min 1 or the spec's.                                                        |
| `non_get_is_none`                                   | Only `GET` is paged.                                                                                                         |
| `lowercase_get_is_detected`                         | A lower-case method is still `GET`.                                                                                          |
| `array_min_max_items_set_total_bounds`              | `minItems`/`maxItems` become the total bounds.                                                                               |
| `min_items_only_raises_both_bounds`                 | `minItems` alone: 200 gives (200, 200), 50 gives (50, 137).                                                                  |
| `max_items_only_lowers_both_bounds`                 | `maxItems` alone: 10 gives (10, 10), 100 gives (23, 100).                                                                    |
| `min_items_above_max_items_wins_like_the_generator` | `minItems` 5 with `maxItems` 2 gives a total of 5.                                                                           |
| `object_without_array_property_is_none`             | An object with no array is not paged.                                                                                        |
| `typeless_object_with_properties_is_paged`          | An object with `properties` and no `type` counts as an object.                                                               |
| `all_of_envelope_is_paged`                          | An `allOf` envelope is merged first.                                                                                         |
| `array_schema_has_array_envelope`                   | An array schema is the array envelope.                                                                                       |
| `scalar_schema_is_none`                             | A scalar response is not paged.                                                                                              |
| `string_cursor_gives_cursor_style`                  | A string-typed cursor is accepted.                                                                                           |
| `string_page_param_is_not_page_style`               | A string page, offset or size is not recognised.                                                                             |
| `page_base_follows_the_declared_minimum_or_default` | `minimum: 0`, or `default: 0` with no minimum above 0, makes the page 0-based; otherwise, and for the other styles, 1-based. |
| `record_cap_keeps_a_page_within_the_value_budget`   | A record of up to 102 values caps a page at 98 records (97 after an object envelope's own 3); light records keep 10 000.     |

### `window.rs` — query and seed to a window

| Test                                            | What it proves                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `absent_params_give_first_page_default_size`    | No parameters: offset 0, default size.                                                                                       |
| `page_n_maps_to_offset`                         | Page n is offset (n - 1) * size.                                                                                             |
| `size_above_maximum_is_clamped`                 | A size over the maximum is clamped.                                                                                          |
| `size_zero_is_400`                              | `limit=0` is a 400.                                                                                                          |
| `size_below_spec_minimum_is_400`                | A size under the spec minimum is a 400.                                                                                      |
| `page_zero_is_400`                              | `page=0` is a 400.                                                                                                           |
| `negative_offset_is_400`                        | A negative offset is a 400.                                                                                                  |
| `non_integer_is_400`                            | A non-integer is a 400.                                                                                                      |
| `only_ascii_digits_are_accepted`                | `+5`, a space and non-ASCII digits are 400s.                                                                                 |
| `page_past_end_is_empty_window`                 | A page past the end is a window, not an error.                                                                               |
| `huge_page_does_not_overflow`                   | A huge page saturates.                                                                                                       |
| `offset_wins_over_page`                         | Offset style ignores a page value.                                                                                           |
| `cursor_round_trip`                             | An encoded cursor decodes to its offset.                                                                                     |
| `forged_cursor_is_400`                          | An unknown token is a 400.                                                                                                   |
| `non_canonical_cursor_is_400`                   | Stray trailing bits and leading zeros in a cursor are 400s.                                                                  |
| `repeated_query_key_takes_the_first`            | The first of a repeated key counts.                                                                                          |
| `total_is_stable_and_in_bounds`                 | The total is the same for a seed and inside its bounds.                                                                      |
| `total_override_wins_and_is_capped_at_10000`    | `total:` wins and is capped at 10 000.                                                                                       |
| `total_respects_min_max_items`                  | The total honours `minItems`/`maxItems`.                                                                                     |
| `size_bounds_are_normalised`                    | min >= 1, max >= min, default within both.                                                                                   |
| `offset_above_u32_says_it_is_too_large`         | Digits past `u32::MAX` are a 400 saying "exceeds the maximum supported value"; a sign is still "not a non-negative integer". |
| `zero_based_page_maps_page_zero_to_offset_zero` | 0-based: page 0, 1, 3 are offsets 0, 10, 30; `page=-1` is a 400; a 1-based route refuses `page=0`.                           |
| `record_cap_shrinks_the_page_size`              | A size above `record_cap` (asked or default) is shrunk to it, never below 1.                                                 |

### `envelope.rs` — the page body

| Test                                                           | What it proves                                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `array_schema_returns_the_slice`                               | An array response is the slice.                                                                                             |
| `object_envelope_fills_meta`                                   | Total, page, size, pages and `hasMore` agree with the window.                                                               |
| `cursor_envelope_fills_next`                                   | A string `nextCursor` is a cursor token, `null` on the last page.                                                           |
| `count_is_the_total_like_drf`                                  | `count` is the total (DRF).                                                                                                 |
| `count_and_a_total_sibling_both_carry_the_total`               | `count` and `totalCount` both carry the total.                                                                              |
| `unknown_siblings_are_generated_as_today`                      | Other fields are generated as without paging.                                                                               |
| `nested_arrays_inside_items_are_untouched`                     | Arrays inside records are not paged.                                                                                        |
| `two_array_props_pages_the_first`                              | Two arrays: one is paged.                                                                                                   |
| `named_array_beats_unnamed`                                    | A list-named array wins over an earlier unnamed one.                                                                        |
| `unnamed_arrays_page_the_first_in_key_order`                   | Without a list name the first in key order is paged.                                                                        |
| `preview_field_is_not_a_prev_meta_field`                       | `preview`/`previewUrl` are generated; `prevCursor` and `prev_page_token` are filled.                                        |
| `min_items_does_not_cost_extra_generation`                     | A big `minItems` does not use up the value budget of the siblings.                                                          |
| `total_pages_when_size_exceeds_total`                          | `totalPages` is 1 when the size exceeds the total.                                                                          |
| `numeric_next_and_prev_are_page_numbers`                       | A numeric next/prev is a page number.                                                                                       |
| `huge_offset_meta_does_not_overflow`                           | An offset of `u32::MAX` does not overflow the page number.                                                                  |
| `string_next_and_previous_are_urls_for_offset_and_page_styles` | DRF style: a string `next`/`previous` is that page's relative URL in the offset and page styles, `null` when there is none. |
| `zero_based_page_numbers_in_meta`                              | 0-based (`minimum: 0`): `page`, `nextPage`, `prevPage` count from 0; `totalPages` is unchanged.                             |

### `links.rs` — `X-Total-Count` and `Link`

| Test                                                   | What it proves                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| `link_has_first_prev_next_last_for_a_middle_page`      | A middle page has all four relations.                  |
| `no_prev_on_first_and_no_next_on_last`                 | Exact links on the first and last pages.               |
| `other_query_params_are_kept_and_paging_ones_replaced` | Other pairs are kept; the index parameter is replaced. |
| `cursor_style_links_use_the_cursor_param`              | Cursor style: only `next`, with a token.               |
| `values_are_percent_encoded`                           | Query values are percent-encoded.                      |
| `empty_dataset_has_first_only_and_total_zero`          | Empty: `first` only, total 0.                          |
| `past_the_end_links_point_back_to_real_pages`          | Past the end: `prev` is clamped, no `next`.            |
| `offset_style_links`                                   | Offset style links.                                    |
| `u32_max_does_not_overflow`                            | `u32::MAX` offsets in every style.                     |
| `offset_style_prev_past_the_end_is_clamped_to_last`    | Offset 95, size 20, total 100: `prev` is offset 80.    |
| `duplicated_index_param_is_replaced_once`              | A repeated index parameter is replaced once.           |
| `size_zero_is_treated_as_one`                          | A size of 0 is treated as 1.                           |
| `cursor_style_with_total_zero_has_no_link`             | Cursor with total 0: no relation, no `link`.           |
| `zero_based_page_links_start_at_page_zero`             | 0-based: `first` is page 0, `last` is pages - 1.       |

### `walk.rs` — the oracle

| Test                                                  | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `walking_all_pages_equals_the_sequence`               | Walking every page equals records `0..N` generated one by one, for each style and several sizes.                                                                                                                |
| `record_i_is_identical_across_sizes_and_styles`       | Record `i` is the same at any size and style.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `heavy_records_shrink_the_page_and_walk_without_gaps` | Records of up to 102 values with `limit=10000`: the page shrinks to 98 records, stays within 10 000 values, and following `rel="next"` (offset and page styles) walks all 250 records with no gap or duplicate. |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/paging_http/` — paged routes over HTTP (D3)

A server loaded from `tests/fixtures/paging.yaml` (list routes of each style, Spring- and DRF-shaped routes, a path-parameterised envelope, a `total:` route, and `/rows` with a `head` of its own). See [mock](files/kaanal-server/mock.md).

| Test                                                | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `first_page_has_default_size_and_headers`           | No query: default size, `x-total-count` and `link`.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `x_total_count_and_link_are_exposed_to_browsers`    | Both are in `Access-Control-Expose-Headers`.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `link_uses_the_raw_request_path`                    | Links use the still-encoded request path.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `head_matches_get_without_body`                     | A `HEAD` has the `GET`'s status and headers and no body.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `a_spec_head_without_paging_params_is_not_paged`    | A spec `head` operation without paging parameters is not paged.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `total_key_overrides_the_total`                     | `total:` sets `x-total-count`.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `a_head_entry_without_total_inherits_the_get_total` | A `HEAD` entry with no `total:` reports its `GET` entry's total and the same `link`.                                                                                                                                         |
| `total_on_an_unpaged_route_warns`                   | `total:` on an unpaged route warns at load; on a paged one it does not.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `offset_page_two_matches_slice_of_full_walk`        | A page equals the matching slice of a full walk.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `invalid_limit_is_400_with_body`                    | `limit=0` is 400 with the exact error body.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `page_past_end_is_200_empty`                        | Past the end: 200, an empty list.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `cursor_walk_reaches_the_end`                       | Following the cursor ends, in at most N/10 + 2 steps.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `unpaged_route_body_is_unchanged`                   | An unpaged route answers as before, with no paging headers.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `zero_and_one_based_page_walks_reach_the_end`       | Spring (`page` minimum 0, `size`, `{content, totalElements, totalPages}`) walks from `page=0`, links start at page 0, `page=-1` is 400; the 1-based `/orders` refuses `page=0` and walks to `hasMore: false`; no duplicates. |
| `drf_next_urls_walk_to_the_end`                     | DRF (`{count, next, previous, results}`): following the body's `next` URL reaches every record; it equals the `link` next.                                                                                                   |
| `path_parameters_seed_and_fill_a_paged_route`       | `/shelves/{shelf}/stock`: each path has its own records, the path parameter fills `shelf`, pages stay slices of one dataset.                                                                                                 |
| `chaos_error_wins_over_paging`                      | A chaos 503 wins over a bad query and has no paging headers.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `pinned_empty_state_returns_the_empty_form`         | An `empty` pin answers `[]` with no paging headers.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `scoped_requests_page_the_same`                     | A scenario scope does not change the page.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `wire_chaos_on_a_paged_route_has_no_paging_headers` | `malformed` and `reset` carry no paging headers.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `success_and_slow_pins_keep_the_paging_headers`     | `success` and `slow` pins still page and keep the headers.                                                                                                                                                                   |
