## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/draft_http/` — the draft API, validate, apply, remap and hardening

See [draft](files/kaanal-server/draft.md) and [control-api](../web/control-api.md#draft).

| Test                                                                        | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `get_draft_returns_the_file_and_put_replaces_it`                            | The draft starts as the file text with its comment, `dirty` false, generation 1; a PUT replaces it and sets `dirty`; putting the applied text back is clean.                                      |
| `a_put_over_the_size_limit_is_400_and_changes_nothing`                      | A text over 1 MiB is 400 naming the limit and leaves the draft alone; exactly 1 MiB is accepted.                                                                                                  |
| `a_put_without_text_is_rejected_and_a_server_without_a_source_starts_empty` | A body with no `text` is a 4xx; with no draft source the draft starts empty.                                                                                                                      |
| `the_draft_routes_need_the_token`                                           | No token, 401.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `a_valid_draft_returns_ok_with_no_diagnostics`                              | Validating the starting draft is `ok` with an empty `diagnostics` and a `warnings` array.                                                                                                         |
| `a_bad_edge_is_reported_with_its_position`                                  | An edge to an unknown node is `ok: false`, with the line of that edge, the path `["edges", 1, "to"]` and a message naming the node.                                                               |
| `a_syntax_error_and_an_empty_draft_are_reported`                            | A broken YAML text has a line; an empty draft is `ok: false` with a message.                                                                                                                      |
| `a_body_text_is_validated_without_being_stored`                             | A body `text` is validated and the stored draft is unchanged.                                                                                                                                     |
| `validate_does_not_change_the_running_system`                               | Validating a valid draft with an extra node leaves `/topology` and the generation as they were.                                                                                                   |
| `apply_swaps_the_routes`                                                    | After Apply a route that was 404 serves, the old one still does, the draft is clean at generation 2, and the topology has the new node.                                                           |
| `a_request_in_flight_finishes_on_the_old_generation`                        | A request running its old 800 ms chain answers 200 after the Apply, which waited for it; the next request runs the new, fast system.                                                              |
| `an_apply_that_cannot_drain_keeps_the_old_system`                           | With a 100 ms budget against a 1.5 s request: 409, the draft kept and dirty, generation 1, admission resumed at once, the request in flight untouched; the same Apply then succeeds.              |
| `a_request_during_the_pause_waits_and_then_uses_the_new_generation`         | A request that arrives while the Apply drains waits for the swap and answers from the new system (404 for a route it no longer has); the one inside answers from the old.                         |
| `an_invalid_draft_is_400_with_diagnostics_and_changes_nothing`              | A bad draft is 400 with the diagnostic path, generation unchanged, the old system serves on; a `timeout_ms` over 60 s is 400.                                                                     |
| `apply_keeps_chaos_by_name_and_loads_the_new_drafts_scenarios`              | Live chaos on a node that survives, and an active scenario the new draft still defines, are kept (the answer counts them); the new draft's scenarios are loaded.                                  |
| `chaos_survives_when_the_node_order_changes`                                | Chaos set on `db` (node 2) still points at `db` after the draft puts it first, and really delays its requests.                                                                                    |
| `chaos_on_a_removed_node_is_dropped_and_reported`                           | Chaos on a node the new draft lacks is gone and listed as `node 'db'`.                                                                                                                            |
| `a_replica_beyond_the_new_count_is_dropped`                                 | A chaos entry on replica 2 is kept at 3 replicas and dropped (`node 'db' replica 2`) at 2.                                                                                                        |
| `a_leased_unit_keeps_its_lease_or_loses_it_with_its_key`                    | A `ttl` lease survives an Apply with its remaining time, and goes with its key when the node is removed.                                                                                          |
| `an_active_scenario_survives_unless_the_new_draft_lacks_it`                 | Of two active scenarios the one still defined stays active, the removed one is dropped and listed.                                                                                                |
| `a_pin_survives_while_its_endpoint_does`                                    | A live pin is kept while its endpoint exists and dropped (listed by endpoint key) when the route is gone.                                                                                         |
| `a_scenario_file_naming_a_removed_node_is_flagged_and_apply_succeeds`       | A `scenarios/*.yaml` naming a removed node does not fail Apply or validate: it is left out and listed in `flagged` with file, scenario and message.                                               |
| `a_runtime_scenario_survives_an_apply_and_yields_to_a_draft_definition`     | A scenario made through the API stays (and stays active) when nodes are reordered, still naming `db`; when the new draft defines the same name, the draft's definition wins and the swap says so. |
| `a_link_cap_is_dropped_when_the_edge_loses_its_bandwidth`                   | A live `bandwidth_cap` is kept while the edge has its bandwidth and removed (latency kept, listed) when the new draft takes the bandwidth away.                                                   |
| `validate_and_apply_agree_when_an_auth_node_has_no_key`                     | A draft that adds an auth node to a server with no signing key is `ok: false` in validate and 400 in apply.                                                                                       |
| `a_draft_edited_during_an_apply_stays_dirty_with_its_new_text`              | A PUT made while an Apply drains is not lost: after it the draft has the new text, is dirty, and is at generation 2.                                                                              |
| `traffic_never_sees_a_gap_across_swaps`                                     | Six clients hammering a route through three Applies get 200 every time.                                                                                                                           |
| `a_draft_of_escapes_is_judged_by_the_text_limit_not_the_body_limit`         | 1 MiB of newlines (2 MiB as JSON) is accepted; one byte more is the documented 400, not a 413.                                                                                                    |
| `an_absolute_spec_path_is_refused_even_when_the_file_exists`                | A draft whose `spec:` is the absolute path of a file that exists is refused with a message naming only the draft's own text, at `nodes.app.spec`.                                                 |
| `a_parent_dir_spec_path_is_refused`                                         | `../x` and `specs/../../x` are refused.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `data_and_endpoints_files_are_held_to_the_project_too`                      | An absolute `data:` and a `../` `endpoints:` are refused, with the path of the key.                                                                                                               |
| `a_missing_or_non_file_spec_reports_a_relative_path_and_no_content`         | A missing spec and a folder named as a spec are reported by their relative path (`not a regular file`), never the project's absolute path.                                                        |
| `a_spec_in_a_subfolder_of_the_project_is_fine_and_apply_refuses_the_rest`   | `specs/api.yaml` validates; Apply of a `../` spec is 400 with the same message.                                                                                                                   |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/draft_http/save.rs` — patches and Save

See [draft](files/kaanal-server/draft.md).

| Test                                                         | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `a_patch_keeps_comments_and_style`                           | A `set`, an `insert` of an object (keys in the order sent) and a `remove` change only their own bytes: the comment and the flow style stay, `lost` is empty and the result still validates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `a_bad_patch_is_400_and_changes_nothing`                     | A missing path, a duplicate key, a path step that is neither key nor index, a missing value, an insert without a key, both or neither of `text` and `patch` are 400; an unknown `op` is the extractor's 422; the draft is as it was.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `the_disk_is_untouched_until_save`                           | A patch and an Apply leave the file's bytes and mtime as they were.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `save_writes_the_draft_and_the_next_save_is_not_a_conflict`  | Save writes the draft, answers `written: ["kaanal.yaml"]`, leaves no temp file, and a second Save after another patch is not a conflict (Save updates the base it compares with).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `save_without_confirm_lists_what_would_be_lost`              | A patch on an anchored value reports `lost`; Save is 409 with the list and writes nothing; with `confirm_loss` it writes the draft as it stands and `lost` clears.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `replacing_the_text_clears_what_was_lost`                    | A whole-text `PUT` empties `lost`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `save_refuses_when_the_file_changed_or_vanished_on_disk`     | A file edited elsewhere is 409 `conflict: true` and keeps the other edit; a deleted file is a conflict and is not recreated.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `save_refuses_a_draft_that_names_a_file_outside_the_project` | An absolute `spec:`, a `../` `data:` and an absolute `endpoints:` are each 400 with the project-folder message and nothing is written; the same paths written with an anchor, an alias or a block scalar are refused too, and so is a path in a draft the loader would reject for another reason (an unknown field), a node whose name is a number or a boolean, a tagged path, and a path that is a list or a map; a relative spec and a draft that is not YAML still save. |
| `save_with_no_config_file_is_409`                            | A server with no config file has nothing to save to.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/draft_http/fix.rs` — Save with fix_scenarios

See [draft](files/kaanal-server/draft.md).

| Test                                                                                 | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `remove_deletes_only_the_flagged_files_and_the_next_load_works`                      | `fix_scenarios: remove` deletes `scenarios/f.yaml` (flagged), keeps `ok.yaml`, reports `removed`, and the saved draft validates with nothing flagged.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `without_a_fix_the_flagged_file_stays`                                               | No body and `none` leave the file.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `rewrite_follows_a_rename_into_the_scenario_files`                                   | After `rename_node db -> store`, `fix_scenarios: rewrite` changes `chaos[].node` and the `edge` end in the flagged file (the comment and layout stay), leaves a file that does not name `db` byte for byte, lists `scenarios/f.yaml` under `written`, and the draft then validates with nothing flagged; the renames are spent (a second rewrite is 400). |
| `renames_chain_and_a_whole_text_put_forgets_them`                                    | `db -> store -> vault` rewrites to `vault`; a whole-text `PUT` clears the recorded renames, so `rewrite` is 400.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `rewrite_without_a_rename_is_400_and_a_file_that_cannot_be_rewritten_stops_the_save` | No recorded rename is 400; a scenario file with the name inside an anchored value is 400 `nothing was saved` and neither it nor `kaanal.yaml` changes.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `a_refused_save_deletes_nothing`                                                     | A conflict (file changed on disk) is 409 and the flagged file stays.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `remove_on_a_draft_that_does_not_build_is_400`                                       | `remove` on a draft that does not build is 400 and deletes nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

## `crates/kaanal-server/tests/draft_http/blank.rs` — blank start and add_node

See [draft](files/kaanal-server/draft.md).

| Test                                                         | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `a_blank_server_serves_only_the_control_api`                 | The mock answers 404 `no system applied yet`; health and the UI work; topology is 409; the draft is `BLANK_TEXT` at generation 0 and dirty.                                                                                                                                   |
| `a_blank_draft_cannot_apply_without_an_entry_node`           | Apply of the blank draft is 400 with a diagnostic, and there is still no system.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `the_first_apply_creates_the_first_generation`               | `add_node` twice, a spec and an edge by patch, then Apply: generation 1, not dirty, topology 200, the spec's route is served; a second Apply is generation 2.                                                                                                                 |
| `add_node_gets_a_default_capacity_and_validates`             | For each of the seven kinds the new node's line holds its default (`concurrency: { limit: 256, queue: 100 }` with `timeout: 30s` on the gateway, `queue: 100` on the service, `proc` for monolith and auth, `size: 20`, `flavor: redis`, `size: 10`) and the draft validates. |
| `add_node_refuses_what_it_cannot_do`                         | An unknown kind, a missing name, a missing kind and a duplicate name are 400 and change nothing.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `a_blank_start_saves_a_new_file_and_refuses_if_one_appeared` | Save creates the file; a file that appears after the start is a 409 conflict, never overwritten.                                                                                                                                                                              |

| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `rename_node_rewrites_every_reference`                            | The `nodes:` key, an edge end, a node chain (bare and `call`), an endpoint chain, an `error_map` key and an inline scenario's node and edge end change; `touched` lists each place; the other end of an edge and the comment stay; the draft still validates. |

| `rename_refuses_when_a_reference_is_in_an_alias`                  | An anchored `edges` list makes the rename 400 `nothing was changed`; the draft is not half renamed.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `rename_refuses_what_makes_no_sense`                              | Unknown node, taken name, same name, empty name and a missing field are 400.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `an_endpoints_file_is_reported_not_silently_left`                 | An `endpoints:` file path gives one note naming both names, and the text keeps the path.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `a_renamed_node_loses_live_chaos_but_inline_scenarios_still_load` | After Apply the chaos on the old name is dropped and reported, the inline scenario (rewritten with the rename) is still defined, and a scenario file outside `kaanal.yaml` is flagged.                                                                        |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/draft/patch.rs` and `save.rs` (unit)

See [draft](files/kaanal-server/draft.md).

| Test                                                                   | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `values_render_in_flow_style`                                          | Numbers, booleans, null, bare words, `{ from: a, to: b, net: 2ms }` in the order sent, `[a, 1]` and `{}`.                                                                                                 |
| `a_null_value_is_a_value_and_a_missing_one_is_not`                     | `"value": null` sets null; no `value` is "needs a value".                                                                                                                                                 |
| `strings_that_would_change_meaning_are_quoted`                         | `yes`, `8`, `a: b`, empty, a newline and `1e3` are quoted; `2ms` and `1.5s` are bare.                                                                                                                     |
| `a_failure_between_write_and_rename_leaves_the_old_file_and_no_temp`   | With a rename that fails, the target keeps its old text and the temp file is gone.                                                                                                                        |
| `every_change_is_made_or_none`                                         | A delete, a replace and a create where the second rename fails: the delete is undone, the replaced file has its old text, the new file is absent, no temp file is left; with no failure all three happen. |
| `a_file_that_cannot_be_read_stops_the_commit_and_restores_what_it_did` | A delete followed by a write onto a folder: the read fails, the delete is undone, the folder stays, no temp file is left.                                                                                 |
| `a_put_during_a_save_is_kept_and_the_saved_text_is_what_was_checked`   | A `PUT` that lands after the snapshot: the checked text is written, the draft keeps the newer one with its `lost`, and the next Save writes it without a conflict.                                        |
| `temp_names_are_unique_and_never_reuse_what_is_there`                  | Two staged temp files differ, and `create_new` refuses a path that already exists.                                                                                                                        |
| `a_plan_that_fails_writes_nothing`                                     | A failing plan is `SaveError::Plan` and the file is untouched.                                                                                                                                            |
| `a_config_that_is_a_link_is_written_through`                           | (unix) the link stays a link and its target gets the text.                                                                                                                                                |
| `a_failed_stage_changes_nothing`                                       | A write into a missing folder fails while staging, before anything is renamed.                                                                                                                            |
| `a_good_write_replaces_the_file_whole_and_leaves_no_temp`              | The new bytes (CRLF included) replace the file and only it is left in the folder.                                                                                                                         |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/chaos_lease.rs` (unit) — the chaos lease table

Pure functions, no server. See [chaos_lease](files/kaanal-server/chaos_lease.md).

| Test                                                      | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `units_set_table`                                         | An empty layer sets no unit; each of `latency_add`, `error_rate`, `error_kind`, `error_status`, `down`, `replace` alone gives its unit (the three error fields all give `error`); a full layer gives all four in order. |
| `apply_some_then_none_removes_only_the_named_units`       | `Some(ttl)` sets `now + ttl` and a second `Some` replaces it; `None` for `down` on one key leaves its `latency` lease and the other key's `down` lease.                                                                 |
| `prune_drops_a_lease_when_the_entry_or_the_field_is_gone` | All three leases stay while the fields are set; after one entry loses `down` and another is removed, only the remaining `latency` lease is left.                                                                        |
| `clear_units_per_unit`                                    | Clearing each unit of a full layer removes exactly that unit (`error` takes rate, kind and status together; `mode` unsets `replace`).                                                                                   |
| `jitter_has_its_own_lease_apart_from_latency`             | Jitter is its own lease unit: clearing it leaves `latency_ms` set.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `remaining_rounds_up_and_lists_only_the_key`              | 1.5 ms left shows as 2, 2 s as 2000, another key's lease is not listed, and a key with none gives an empty map.                                                                                                         |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/shutdown.rs` (unit) — the deadline handle

On tokio's paused clock, so they take no real time. See [shutdown](files/kaanal-server/shutdown.md).

| Test                                                               | What it proves                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `first_request_fixes_the_deadline_and_later_ones_do_not_extend_it` | `request(5 s)` returns 5 s; 2 s later `request(60 s)` returns the 3 s left and `remaining` agrees; past the deadline `request(1 s)` returns zero. |
| `remaining_is_none_before_a_request`                               | A fresh handle has no deadline.                                                                                                                   |
| `requested_resolves_after_request`                                 | A task waiting on `requested()` is still pending until `request` is called, then finishes.                                                        |
| `expired_resolves_at_the_deadline`                                 | After `request(5 s)`, `expired()` resolves exactly 5 s later.                                                                                     |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/log.rs` (unit) — ids, the ring and the filters

| Test                                                                 | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `id_grammar_round_trips`                                             | `format_id(0x4f2, 42)` is `r04f2-0042`, a five-digit number is kept, and `parse_id` rejects `r04f2-42`, uppercase hex, the three-digit boot `r4f2-0042`, a wrong prefix and a non-digit.                     |
| `ids_increase_and_start_at_one`                                      | A generator with boot `abcd` issues `(1, "rabcd-0001")`, then 2.                                                                                                                                             |
| `client_id_grammar`                                                  | `abc-1.2_x` passes; a space, a newline, 65 bytes and the empty string do not; 64 bytes do.                                                                                                                   |
| `ring_keeps_the_last_1000_by_completion_and_counts_evictions`        | 1005 pushes keep 1000, `evicted` is 5, `oldest` is `...0006`, `newest` `...1005`, and `find` of `...0005` is `Evicted`.                                                                                      |
| `out_of_order_pushes_keep_seq_and_done_separate`                     | Pushing seq 5, 3, 4 gives `done` 1, 2, 3 and `oldest`/`newest` by `seq`; `after=1` returns seq 3 and 4.                                                                                                      |
| `find_evicted_vs_unknown_with_out_of_order_completion`               | With seq 1 never completed and 2 to 1002 pushed, ids 1 and 2 are `Evicted` (below the high-water mark), 3 is found, another boot and a number above the mark are `Unknown`, and `not valid!` is `Malformed`. |
| `late_low_seq_after_eviction_is_evicted_not_unknown`                 | A slow seq 1 landing after 1000 others evicts seq 2 (the earliest to finish) and is itself found.                                                                                                            |
| `eviction_drops_the_earliest_done_not_the_lowest_seq`                | The cap drops by completion order: the late low seq 1 stays while 2, 3 and 4 go.                                                                                                                             |
| `find_precedence_and_leading_zero_alias`                             | A client id shaped like an id resolves as the id; `r04f2-00002` and `r04f2-0002` are the same entry.                                                                                                         |
| `find_last_and_client_id_newest_wins`                                | `last` on an empty ring is `Unknown`; afterwards it is the highest `done`; two entries sharing a client id resolve to the newer; an unsent id is `Unknown`.                                                  |
| `clear_keeps_counters`                                               | After `clear` the ring is empty, the old id is `Evicted` and the next `done` is 3 (not 1).                                                                                                                   |
| `filter_status_exact_class_list`                                     | `404`, `5xx` and `404,5xx` match as exact, class and list.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `filter_failed_scope_since_after`                                    | `failed`, an exact `scope`, `since` as a duration and as an RFC 3339 time, `after`, and a lower-case endpoint normalised to `GET /orders/{id}`.                                                              |
| `filter_empty_scope_is_distinct_from_no_scope`                       | The empty-scope filter matches only an entry whose scope is `Some("")`, not `None`; no filter matches both.                                                                                                  |
| `filter_rejects_unknown_empty_and_malformed_params_naming_the_param` | Twelve bad pairs (`bogus`, empty `status`, `9xx`, `99`, `failed=yes`, `since=yesterday`, `after=-1`, `endpoint=orders`, empty endpoint, `+5`, `404`, `+404`) each fail with a message naming the key.        |
| `limit_is_ignored_and_duplicates_rejected`                           | `limit` alone yields the default filter; a repeated `limit`, `scope` or `after` is an error naming it.                                                                                                       |
| `empty_scope_param_filters_the_empty_header_scope`                   | `scope=` returns only the entry with the empty-header scope out of none, empty and `a`.                                                                                                                      |
| `query_returns_last_limit_oldest_first`                              | Five entries, `limit` 2: the last two, in order.                                                                                                                                                             |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/record/tests.rs` (unit) — trace text, flags and hops

| Test                                                             | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `header_equals_cap_of_full_trace_for_every_existing_trace_case`  | A small trace has equal `header` and `full`, with the error and `pinned=error` tails; 400 hops cut the header at 4096 bytes (ending `hops,pinned=slow`, a prefix of the whole log copy); 2000 hops cut the log copy at 16 KiB with the same rule; `entry.trace == parts.full`.                 |
| `structured_hops_rerender_to_the_trace_text_while_not_truncated` | A plan with a two-branch block, a cache miss, a delay and a queued hop: the structured `hops` rebuilt into text equal the log's trace text (`[app.proc=2.0ms\|db.wait=2.0ms,db.miss=3.0ms,db.delay=5.0ms]`). Holds for one level of `parallel`; nested blocks flatten to the innermost lane.   |
| `hops_over_512_set_truncated`                                    | 513 hops keep 512 and set `truncated`; exactly 512 do not.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `flags_per_cause`                                                | `Chaos` gives `FI`, `RateLimit` `RL`, `Overflow` `UO`, `Deadline` `DT`, none no flag; a pin delay gives `DI`, `PD`, `PN` with the first hop noted `pin delay`; a hang (a delay hop then the node's own timeout failure) is `FI` and never `DI`, with no note.                                  |
| `a_cache_hang_is_a_fault_not_injected_delay`                     | A cache lookup charged `HANG_CAP` (or its node `timeout`) under a `hang` fault succeeds as `FI` with the note `chaos hang`; the same lookup under plain latency is `DI` with `chaos +40ms`; a lookup cut to its timeout with no hang fault has no flag and no note.                            |
| `a_deadline_cut_hop_never_reports_more_than_it_ran`              | 300 ms of chaos on a hop the deadline cut to 150 ms is noted `chaos +150ms`, and the hop is `cut`.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `chain_error_survives_error_map_and_pin`                         | Under a `success` pin the entry has status 200, no failed node and `chain_error { db, 503, status, chaos }`; with an `error_map` status of 502 the chain error still says 503; the causes name themselves (`rate-limit`, `overflow`, `deadline`); a reset keeps kind `reset` even under a pin. |
| `lanes_for_a_parallel_block`                                     | Hops of a two-branch block are `(1, fork 0)` and `(2, fork 0)`, the hops around it `(0, none)`.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `a_hop_shorter_than_planned_is_cut`                              | A 10 ms hop that ran 4 ms is `cut` with `dur_ms` 4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/mock/tests.rs` (unit) — ids, the log and the event

These drive `handle` directly against a state with a live engine and read the ring through `st.log`. Chaos comes from scenarios activated by the `X-Kaanal-Scenario` header (`down`, `lag`, `hidden`, `hang` ...).

| Test                                                                  | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `a_mock_with_a_narrow_link_delays_the_response`                       | A 1000 B/s edge and `Content-Length: 50`: the log entry has a `link` hop of 50 ms, then a `link-resp` hop, the trace has `db.link=50`, and the client waited at least 50 ms.                                                                                       |
| `no_payload_costs_means_no_link_hops_whatever_the_body`               | Without any `bandwidth` or `per_kb`, a 5000-byte request makes no link hop (the topology does not even look at sizes).                                                                                                                                             |
| `a_head_response_carries_no_response_transfer`                        | A HEAD on a narrow link has the request link hop and no response one.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `each_request_makes_exactly_one_entry_and_one_event`                  | Two good requests, a `404`, a `405`, a bad scope, a scoped chaos failure and an `OPTIONS` preflight: six entries and six events, the entry ids equal the response ids in order, all distinct; the preflight made none.                                             |
| `unmatched_and_bad_scope_are_logged_with_rejected`                    | A `404`, a `405` and a bad scope are logged as `(404, unmatched, no endpoint, 0 hops)`, `(405, unmatched, ...)` and `(400, bad-scope, GET /users/{id}, 0 hops)`; none has a trace or a failed node; each has an event with the same `rejected` and no nodes.       |
| `a_rejected_request_does_not_touch_the_counter`                       | After a good request, a bad scope, a `404` and a `405` leave the sum of the route counters unchanged but make three more entries.                                                                                                                                  |
| `secrets_are_never_stored`                                            | A body, an `Authorization` and a custom header, and a query token: none of the three secrets is in the serialised entries or events; the client id and the clean path are.                                                                                         |
| `pinned_request_keeps_chain_error`                                    | A `success` pin over a failing database: status 200, `pin` `success`, no failed node, `chain_error { db, 503, status, chaos }`, trace ending `db.error=503,pinned=success`, flags `FI`, `PN`. Without the pin the same failure is a `503` with `failed_node` `db`. |
| `out_of_order_completion_lands`                                       | A 300 ms request started first is not in the ring while three fast ones finish; afterwards it is last with `seq` 1 and `done` 4, flagged `DI`, its `read` hop noted `chaos +300ms`.                                                                                |
| `concurrent_requests_all_recorded_once`                               | 50 tasks (every fifth a `404`): 50 entries, `seq` and `done` each exactly 1 to 50.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `reset_empties_the_ring_but_ids_keep_counting`                        | After `control::reset` the ring is empty and the next id ends `-0002`.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `engine_stopped_is_logged_with_an_id`                                 | With no engine, a request answers `500`, with an id; one entry `engine-stopped` with the endpoint and exactly one event of the same id and status 500.                                                                                                             |
| `a_dropped_request_leaves_one_client_gone_entry`                      | A request held 150 ms by a hang, abandoned at 40 ms: one entry with status 499, `client-gone`, the endpoint, no hops and no trace, and one event; 250 ms later nothing more is logged; a request that completes afterwards is not marked `client-gone`.            |
| `a_hang_is_never_reported_as_injected_delay`                          | A hang on a node with a `timeout`: `504`, flags exactly `DT`, no hop notes, `chain_error.kind` `timeout`.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `injected_latency_is_attributed_on_nodes_caches_and_endpoints`        | Endpoint chaos (20 ms) notes the `delay` hop, cache chaos (40 ms) the `hit` hop, both flagged `DI`; the database hop is an `op` of `read`.                                                                                                                         |
| `a_pin_over_a_reset_keeps_the_reset_kind`                             | A `reset` at the database under a `success` pin answers 200 with `chain_error { db, reset, chaos }` and `db.error=reset` in the trace.                                                                                                                             |
| `long_paths_and_methods_are_capped_in_the_log`                        | A 3000-byte path (ending in a two-byte character) is stored at most 2048 bytes and ends `…`; a 24-byte method at most 16 bytes; a short path is kept whole.                                                                                                        |
| `a_hang_on_a_node_without_a_timeout_is_fault_injection`               | Paused time: a hang on a node with no `timeout:` is a `504`, flags exactly `FI`, no hop notes, `chain_error { timeout, chaos }`.                                                                                                                                   |
| `a_blank_state_answers_404_and_a_system_answers_again_once_installed` | With no generation a mock request is a 404 with no id, counter or log entry; installing one serves again.                                                                                                                                                          |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/scope.rs` (unit) — the scope header and cache

| Test                        | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `parse_rows`                | No header is `None`; an empty or blank value is an explicit empty stack; names are trimmed; `a,b,a` is `[b, a]`; 16 names pass and 17 fail (17 repeats of one name fail too); `a,,b`, `a,`, `,a`, `Bad!`, a non-ASCII byte and 257 bytes fail; two header lines fail. |
| `cache_clears_at_the_bound` | Compiling more than 256 distinct stacks keeps the cache at 256 or fewer and the last stack compiled is in it.                                                                                                                                                         |

## `crates/kaanal-server/src/auth.rs` (unit) — key, JWKS and tokens

Each test shares one 2048-bit key per test binary (generation is the slow part).

| Test                                                          | What it proves                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `thumbprint_matches_rfc_7638`                                 | The thumbprint of the RFC 7638 section 3.1 example key is `NzbLsXh8uDCcd-6MNwXF4W_7noWXFZAfHkxZsRGC9Xs`.                                      |
| `jwks_has_one_rs256_signing_key_named_by_its_thumbprint`      | The JWKS has exactly one key with `kty`, `use`, `alg`, `kid`, `n` (256 bytes), `e` = `AQAB`; the `kid` is the thumbprint of that `n`/`e`.     |
| `pem_round_trips_and_debug_hides_the_key`                     | PKCS#8 PEM round-trips to the same `kid`; `Debug` shows the `kid` only; garbage is a message that never contains key text.                    |
| `token_verifies_with_an_independent_verifier`                 | `jsonwebtoken` (dev-only) verifies a token against the served JWKS; header `alg`/`typ`/`kid` are right; a swapped payload no longer verifies. |
| `claims_follow_the_precedence`                                | `sub`, `iss`, `iat`, `exp`, `jti` (32 hex), `roles`, `scope` (only with scopes), node claims, then user claims, which win.                    |
| `standard_claims_win_even_if_a_config_skipped_the_load_check` | A hand-built config with `iss` on the node and `sub`/`exp` on a user still gets the real `sub`, `iss` and `exp` in the token.                 |
| `login_rejects_unknown_users_and_wrong_passwords`             | Unknown user, wrong or empty password, wrong case: `None` (401); a good login has 4 fields (3 without scopes) and a random `jti`.             |
| `from_config_fails_closed_without_a_key`                      | An auth node and no key is a message naming the node; no auth node is `None`; defaults are a 1 h ttl and `iss` from the bound port.           |

## `crates/kaanal/src/keys.rs` (unit) — the signing key file

| Test                                                          | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `keys_dir_prefers_the_override_and_needs_one_of_them`         | `KAANAL_CONFIG_DIR` wins over the local config dir, an empty value counts as unset, and neither is an exit-1 error that names `KAANAL_CONFIG_DIR`.                                     |
| `key_id_hashes_the_canonical_config_directory`                | The id is 16 hex digits of the SHA-256 of the canonical directory (same directory by any path gives the same id, another directory another id).                                        |
| `store_adopts_the_key_that_linked_first`                      | A second `store` for the same name returns the first key and leaves no temp file; mode 0600 on Unix.                                                                                   |
| `the_no_hard_link_fallback_creates_once_and_never_overwrites` | The direct-create fallback used when hard links are unsupported creates the key once (0600 on Unix), reports `AlreadyExists` for a second attempt, and leaves the winner's key intact. |
| `a_corrupt_key_file_is_an_error_naming_it`                    | Text that is not a key is an error naming the file, with the delete hint; the file is not replaced.                                                                                    |

## `crates/kaanal/src/init.rs` (unit) and `crates/kaanal/tests/init.rs` — `kaanal init`

| Test                                                                     | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `render_config_is_a_valid_project` (render.rs)                           | The output parses; header first; nodes gateway, service, database, cache with flavors; three edges with a `net`; no `hold`; estimate comment.                                                                                 |
| `the_numbers_are_the_studio_starter_numbers` (render.rs)                 | The generated gateway and event-loop app nodes equal `presets::starter` (apart from flavor label and spec).                                                                                                                   |
| `thread_per_request_holds_across_calls_with_200_slots` (render.rs)       | Thread per request writes `hold: call` and `concurrency: { limit: 200, queue: 100 }`, and still parses.                                                                                                                       |
| `a_spec_path_with_quotes_stays_one_string` (render.rs)                   | Quotes and backslashes in the spec path round-trip through the YAML.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `endpoints_are_chosen_by_method_and_path` (render.rs)                    | GET collection is a scan, GET ending in `{param}` (also `{*rest}`, or a trailing slash after it) a read through the cache, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE a write; HEAD and OPTIONS get no entry; no `per_kb`.                      |
| `a_spec_of_only_head_and_options_has_no_endpoints_block` (render.rs)     | No entry to write: no `endpoints:` key, still a valid config.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `the_peak_profile_has_a_journey_per_endpoint_and_at_most_64` (render.rs) | The `peak` profile has a journey per `endpoints:` entry (GET 3, others 1, none for HEAD); 70 routes give 70 entries but 64 journeys, and the file still loads.                                                                |
| `the_summary_names_the_style_and_the_numbers` (render.rs)                | The `Chose: ...` line for both styles.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `the_fourth_question_is_the_server_style`                                | The exact prompt text, `[event-loop]` default on Enter, a typed value, end of input is the default.                                                                                                                           |
| `a_bad_server_style_answer_is_asked_again_and_three_fail`                | A bad answer is explained (naming both values) and re-asked; three bad answers exit 64.                                                                                                                                       |
| `the_server_style_flag_answers_its_question_and_a_bad_one_is_refused`    | `--server-style` removes its question; a bad value is 64 at once and never re-asked.                                                                                                                                          |
| `prompts_use_defaults_on_empty_lines`                                    | Empty lines take the shown defaults; prompts show `[default]`.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `prompts_take_typed_answers_and_end_of_input_is_the_default`             | Typed answers win; running out of input falls back to defaults.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `flags_answer_their_prompt_so_nothing_is_asked`                          | A flag removes exactly its own question.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `yes_and_non_interactive_take_defaults_without_reading_input`            | `--yes` or no terminal asks nothing and reads nothing; no spec then is a usage error.                                                                                                                                         |
| `bad_flags_are_hard_usage_errors`                                        | Bad service, port or locale flags exit 64 and are never re-asked.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `a_bad_typed_answer_is_explained_and_asked_again`                        | A bad prompted answer prints why and asks again.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `three_bad_answers_fail_with_the_usage_error`                            | Three bad answers: usage 64 with the last reason.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `end_of_input_midway_takes_the_defaults`                                 | End of input gives the defaults for what is left.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `gitignore_is_appended_to_in_its_own_line_ending`                        | Created, LF, CRLF and a missing final newline are appended to, never rewritten.                                                                                                                                               |
| `existing_ignore_lines_add_nothing`                                      | Each of the six equivalent lines counts as already ignored; file unchanged.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `a_bom_on_the_first_gitignore_line_does_not_hide_the_entry`              | A UTF-8 BOM before `.kaanal/` still counts as listed; no duplicate.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `a_port_is_plain_digits`                                                 | `+80`, `-1`, `0`, 65536, empty, spaces and `0x50` are refused; `0080` is 80.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `an_unreadable_gitignore_is_left_alone`                                  | A UTF-16 file and a directory give `Err`; bytes unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `init_writes_kaanal_yaml_and_appends_gitignore_once`                     | Config and `.gitignore` written; a second run adds no duplicate line; no temp file left.                                                                                                                                      |
| `init_refuses_an_existing_kaanal_yaml_without_force`                     | Exit 64 naming `--force`; the folder is unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `force_overwrites`                                                       | `--force` replaces the file.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `a_service_name_with_yaml_metacharacters_is_rejected`                    | Bad `--service` values exit 64 and write nothing.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `init_writes_real_numbers_and_an_endpoint_per_route`                     | The file validates; 256/32 slots, queue 100, 2 ms, 25 ms, `net` on every edge, 30 s timeout, an entry per route by method, no `hold`; stdout is exactly `Wrote`, `Chose: ...`, `Next`.                                        |
| `init_writes_a_peak_profile_with_one_journey_per_endpoint`               | `traffic.peak` is `rps: 100`, `duration: 30s`, one journey per `endpoints:` key (GET 3, others 1), with an estimate comment; the file validates.                                                                              |
| `the_generated_peak_profile_runs_in_the_simulator`                       | The profile run through the simulator library against the generated file: requests above 0, none failed.                                                                                                                      |
| `a_head_and_options_only_spec_gets_no_traffic_section`                   | No journey to write: no `traffic:` key, still valid.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `an_unusable_gitignore_is_a_warning_and_init_still_succeeds`             | A directory or UTF-16 `.gitignore`: warning on stderr, exit 0, kaanal.yaml written, file untouched.                                                                                                                           |
| `a_crlf_gitignore_keeps_crlf`                                            | The appended line ends in CRLF.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `an_existing_temp_file_is_refused_and_left_alone`                        | `kaanal.yaml.tmp` present: exit 64, file unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `errors_name_kaanal_yaml_not_the_temp_file`                              | A check failure names `kaanal.yaml`, never `.tmp`.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `non_interactive_without_a_spec_is_a_usage_error_64`                     | No spec and no terminal: 64, folder unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `yes_and_non_interactive_never_block`                                    | With piped stdin that never sends a line, init still finishes.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `bad_specs_leave_the_folder_unchanged`                                   | A 2.0 spec, a spec with no paths and a missing spec exit 1 and leave no files.                                                                                                                                                |
| `fetches_a_spec_over_http_loopback`                                      | A loopback `http` URL: saved as `shop.yaml` (name from the path, query ignored), referenced by the config, `validate` passes.                                                                                                 |
| `rejects_http_to_a_non_loopback_host`                                    | Plain `http` to a non-loopback name or address, and an `ftp://` URL: 64, nothing written, no request.                                                                                                                         |
| `follows_up_to_three_redirects_and_stops_at_the_fourth`                  | One absolute and then relative `Location`s: three are followed; a fourth fails with exit 1 and leaves no file.                                                                                                                |
| `rejects_html_that_is_not_openapi`                                       | An HTML 200: exit 1 "not an OpenAPI 3.0 document", nothing written.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `error_text_names_the_url_not_the_body`                                  | A 200 that is not a spec and a 500: the error names the URL without its query and never the body or its escapes.                                                                                                              |
| `a_failed_check_removes_the_downloaded_spec`                             | A spec with no paths passes the fetch but fails the project check: exit 1, the download is removed.                                                                                                                           |
| `url_with_userinfo_is_a_usage_error`                                     | `user:password@` or `user@` in the URL: 64, the password is not echoed.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `refuses_to_overwrite_an_existing_spec_without_force`                    | An existing file of that name: 64 naming `--force`; forced but failing `check`: restored from the backup, no warning, no backup left; a leftover `.kaanal-old`: 64, both kept; forced: replaced, output says so, backup gone. |
| `a_loopback_fetch_ignores_environment_proxies`                           | `HTTP_PROXY`/`ALL_PROXY` set to a dead address: a loopback `http` fetch still succeeds.                                                                                                                                       |
| `a_redirect_to_a_forbidden_host_is_refused`                              | A loopback server redirecting to plain `http` on a remote host: exit 1 "redirect refused" naming it, nothing written.                                                                                                         |
| `without_the_https_feature_an_https_url_is_a_usage_error`                | (`--no-default-features` only) `https://`: 64 naming the `'https' feature`.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `rejects_an_https_to_http_redirect` (fetch.rs)                           | The redirect policy refuses `https` to `http`, allows loopback `http` to `http` and `https` to `https`.                                                                                                                       |
| `plain_http_reaches_loopback_hosts_only` (fetch.rs)                      | `localhost`, `127.0.0.0/8`, `[::1]` pass; other hosts, `0.0.0.0`, IPv4-mapped loopback, other schemes and userinfo fail.                                                                                                      |
| `shown_drops_userinfo_query_and_fragment_and_escapes` (fetch.rs)         | Message URLs lose userinfo, query and fragment, even when unparseable; control characters are escaped.                                                                                                                        |
| `the_file_name_comes_from_the_last_segment` (fetch.rs)                   | Decoded, reduced to `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`; traversal, no extension, a leading dot and `kaanal.yaml` give `openapi.yaml`.                                                                                                           |
| `rejects_a_body_over_10_mb` (fetch.rs)                                   | A body with no `Content-Length` one byte over a small test cap fails; `MAX_BODY` is 10 MiB.                                                                                                                                   |
| `times_out_a_silent_server` (fetch.rs)                                   | A server that never answers fails within the short test budget; `TIMEOUT` is 10 s.                                                                                                                                            |
| `times_out_a_slow_drip_body` (fetch.rs)                                  | A body sent a byte every 50 ms runs out of the budget while being read: reported as a timeout.                                                                                                                                |
| `the_deadline_is_shared_across_hops` (fetch.rs)                          | Two redirect hops of 250 ms each exceed a 400 ms budget that either fits alone.                                                                                                                                               |
| `device_names_and_long_names_fall_back` (fetch.rs)                       | `con`, `PRN`, `nul.x`, `com1`, `lpt9` (any case, any extension) and names over 100 characters give `openapi.yaml`; `com0`, `com10`, `console` do not.                                                                         |
| `alias_expansion_in_a_spec_is_bounded` (fetch.rs)                        | A small "billion laughs" YAML is refused by `serde_yaml_ng` ("repetition limit exceeded") in well under 2 s.                                                                                                                  |
| `json_prints_the_written_paths`                                          | `--json` prints `{"written":[...]}`.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `json_with_piped_stdin_prints_exactly_the_object`                        | `--json init` without `--yes` on piped stdin: stdout is exactly `{"written":[...]}` and a newline.                                                                                                                            |
| `init_output_validates_and_starts`                                       | The result passes `validate`, `up` serves `GET /users`, and `endpoints` lists the routes.                                                                                                                                     |

## `crates/kaanal/tests/demo/` — the demo project and `kaanal demo`

One test target (`cargo test -p kaanal --test demo`), one module per topic in `tests/demo/` (R3.8):

| Module         | What it covers                                                                                    |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `common.rs`    | Shared helpers: the binary, project folders, `Up`, the HTTP agent.                                |
| `template.rs`  | The embedded template validates, the page and its assets are served, the page keeps to its rules. |
| `chaos_bar.rs` | What the chaos bar sends is accepted by a live `kaanal up`.                                       |
| `stories.rs`   | Every story has a visible effect; checkout stores an order only when the payment goes through.    |
| `run.rs`       | `kaanal demo` itself: startup, banner, the folder it leaves, locale and port.                     |
| `eject.rs`     | `kaanal demo --eject`: what it writes, refuses and leaves alone.                                  |

The template tests write `FILES` (included from `src/commands/demo/files.rs`) to a scratch folder and run plain `kaanal` on it; the `kaanal demo` tests run the command in an empty folder and stop it with `kaanal down`.

| Test                                                               | What it proves                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `the_template_validates`                                           | The written template passes `kaanal validate`.                                                                                                                              |
| `every_pinned_route_and_scenario_exists`                           | Every route has an explicit chain, `POST /login` works for `demo@example.com`, every story is listed, and the pinned chaos lines are in their files.                        |
| `cart_adds_lists_and_removes_over_http`                            | The cart collection round-trip, store-shaped products 1 to 6, and the page at `/`.                                                                                          |
| `the_template_has_no_real_names_or_brands`                         | Every email in the template is at `example.com`.                                                                                                                            |
| `the_page_and_its_assets_are_served_from_root`                     | `/`, `app.js`, `style.css` and the image are served with their types and embedded bytes.                                                                                    |
| `every_script_style_and_image_the_page_references_is_embedded`     | Every relative reference in the page, script, style and spec is in `FILES`.                                                                                                 |
| `the_page_makes_no_external_requests`                              | No external host in the site files; every `fetch` goes to this origin.                                                                                                      |
| `the_script_builds_no_html_and_keeps_the_token_in_session_storage` | No `innerHTML`, `eval` or `localStorage`; the token is stored once, in `sessionStorage`, and only sent as `Authorization`.                                                  |
| `every_id_the_script_looks_up_is_in_the_page`                      | Each id `app.js` looks up exists in `index.html`.                                                                                                                           |
| `asset_budget`                                                     | The embedded template is under 300 KB.                                                                                                                                      |
| `the_chaos_bar_names_what_the_project_has`                         | The bar's ttl, pinned endpoint and pin states; stories come from the API, each with a one-line description.                                                                 |
| `the_chaos_bar_requests_are_accepted_by_a_live_up`                 | Every scenario and pin call the bar makes is accepted and visible, and refused without the token.                                                                           |
| `the_chaos_sliders_and_reset_are_accepted_by_a_live_up`            | The sliders' chaos calls and "Reset all chaos" work on a live instance.                                                                                                     |
| `every_story_has_a_visible_effect`                                 | Each story changes status or timing on the routes the page uses; the page still loads with the gateway down.                                                                |
